  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作研究多关注抓取和移动物体，但缺乏对物体内部机制（如按压、旋转）与外部功能应用（如喷射、涂抹）相结合的复杂操作能力。这类组合式灵巧功能性物体操作（CD-FOM）任务需要语义理解与物理灵巧性的深度融合，目前尚无有效的零示范解决方案。

Method: CoDex采用零示范框架，分三阶段：1）利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）从任务和场景中推理出语义约束（如抓取位置、施力方向、应用区域）；2）基于语义约束进行解析约束优化，生成少量功能性抓取候选；3）通过强化学习（RL）对候选策略进行细化，学习完整的抓取-移动-驱动（grasp-move-actuate）策略，最终从仿真迁移到真实世界。

Result: 在7自由度机器人臂与16自由度多指手上，对6种CD-FOM任务（喷雾瓶、热胶枪、喷气除尘罐、手电筒、胡椒研磨器等）及其作用于不同目标物体上进行评估，成功展示了无需人类示范即可自主发现并执行复杂的、物理可行的灵巧行为。

Conclusion: CoDex证明了利用VLM语义引导与强化学习相结合的方式，可以有效解决零示范条件下组合式灵巧功能性物体操作任务，为机器人自主操作具有内部机制的未见过物体开辟了新路径。

Abstract: In this work, we study Compositional Dexterous Functional Object Manipulation (CD-FOM): tasks such as aiming and actuating a spray bottle on a plant or a glue gun on wood, which require both actuating an object's internal mechanism and controlling its pose to apply the object's function to the environment. These tasks pose significant challenges for robots due to the demanding integration of semantic understanding of the object's function, actuation mode, and application area with intricate physical dexterity to manage grasp stability, movement trajectory, and actuation. We introduce CoDex, a zero-demonstration framework that autonomously discovers CD-FOM manipulation strategies. CoDex uses vision-language models (VLMs) to infer semantic constraints from the task and scene. These constraints guide analytic constrained optimization to generate a short list of functional grasp candidates that can be efficiently refined with reinforcement learning to generate full grasp-move-actuate policies transferable from simulation to the real world. We evaluate CoDex on a 7-DoF robot arm with a 16-DoF multi-fingered hand across six CD-FOM tasks involving previously unseen objects with internal mechanisms, including spray bottles, hot glue guns, air dusters, flashlights, and pepper grinders, and their application to unseen target objects, showcasing its ability to autonomously discover and execute complex, physically viable dexterous behaviors without human demonstrations. More information at https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/CoDex/.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Locomotion on Discrete Terrain via Minimal Proximity Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31912)
*Jiale Fan,Connor Flynn,Tianao Xu,Junzhe He,Andrei Cramariuc,Marco Hutter,Robert Baines*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在四足机器人足底嵌入低成本、高频红外接近传感器，通过强化学习框架利用触地前的局部感知信号，使机器人能够提前预判地形不连续性（如间隙、垫脚石），在离散地形上显著提升了行走鲁棒性，为传统视觉感知提供了低功耗、低延迟的替代或补充方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局感知系统（如LiDAR、深度相机）存在延迟、遮挡和高计算成本问题，而本体感知反馈仅能在触地后做出反应，两者均无法在接触发生前有效感知小尺度地形变化。本文旨在探索一种轻量级、低成本的近场传感方案，弥补这一感知缺口。

Method: 在四足机器人足部嵌入低成本、高频红外接近传感器，提供触地前的局部距离反馈；将该信号集成到强化学习框架中训练运动策略；在仿真中建模传感器并实现sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 实验表明，足部接近传感显著提升了机器人在离散地形（如间隙、垫脚石）上的行走鲁棒性；该稀疏近场传感方案可在仿真中可靠建模并高保真迁移到真实环境。

Conclusion: 局部接近传感为复杂全局感知系统提供了一种低功耗、低延迟的有效替代或补充方案，尤其适用于不可预测环境中的四足 locomotion 任务。

Abstract: Learning-based control has revolutionized dynamic locomotion, yet navigating unstructured terrain remains limited by a robot's incomplete awareness of imminent ground contact. While global perception systems such as LiDARs and depth cameras provide environmental context, they are frequently plagued by latencies, occlusions, and the high computational cost of dense geometric reconstruction. On the other hand, proprioceptive feedback is purely reactive, initiating corrections only after impact has occurred. This work explores embedding a minimal suite of low-cost, high-frequency infrared proximity sensors directly into the feet of a quadrupedal robot. These sensors provide "pre-contact" feedback that is robust to self-occlusions and significantly less computationally demanding than conventional vision-based pipelines. By integrating these localized signals into a reinforcement learning framework, we enable the robot to anticipate terrain discontinuities such as gaps and stepping stones that are problematic for traditional perception stacks due to occlusions or state estimation drift. We demonstrate that such sparse, near-field sensing can be reliably modeled in simulation and transferred to the real world with high fidelity. Experimental results show that local proximity sensing substantially improves traversal robustness over discrete terrain and offers a low-power, low-latency alternative or complement to complex global perception suites in unpredictable environments. For more information about results and methods, please see the project website: https://sites.google.com/view/foot-tof/home.

</details>


### [98] [MVP-Nav: Multi-layer Value Map Planner Navigator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31919)
*Wenyuan Xie,Shaokai Wu,Yijin Zhou,Yanbiao Ji,Guodong Zhang,Bayram Bayramli,Qiuchang Li,Xunchu Zhou,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVP-Nav提出了一种基于物理感知的RGB-only零样本目标导航框架，通过3D基础模型从单目观测中重建物理占据信息，并引入多层价值图（MVM）统一语义推理与物理约束，在无深度传感器条件下实现了最先进的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-only零样本目标导航中，缺乏显式深度信息导致物理不确定性大、语义-物理不对齐，现有方法要么仅有高层语义推理缺乏几何基础，要么学习端到端策略缺少显式物理约束，行为虽语义合理但物理不安全。

Method: 利用3D基础模型将2D语义实例投影为3D有向边界框，重建显式物理占据信息并构建全局空间语义表示；提出多层价值图（MVM），将语义优先级与重建几何信息集成到共享代价空间中，实现物理有根据的几何规划。

Result: 在零样本目标导航基准上显著优于现有深度无关方法，达到最先进水平，验证了结构化物理先验可以有效补偿主动深度传感器的缺失。

Conclusion: 结构化物理先验能够有效替代主动深度传感器，实现RGB-only条件下的物理感知导航，对齐感知、规划与控制与真实3D世界。

Abstract: Zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ZSON) with RGB-only perception poses a fundamental challenge for embodied agents, as the absence of explicit depth information introduces severe physical uncertainty and semantic-physical misalignment. Existing approaches either rely on high-level semantic reasoning without geometric grounding or learn end-to-end policies that lack explicit physical constraints, often resulting in semantically plausible but physically unsafe behaviors. In this paper, we propose MVP-Nav, a physical-aware RGB-only navigation framework that aligns perception, planning, and control with the real 3D world. MVP-Nav reconstructs explicit physical occupancy from monocular observations by leveraging 3D foundation models to project 2D semantic instances into 3D oriented bounding boxes, forming a global spatial semantic representation. To unify high-level semantic reasoning and low-level physical constraints, we introduce a Multi-layer Value Map (MVM) that integrates semantic priorities and reconstructed geometry into a shared cost space, enabling physically grounded geometric planning. Extensive experiments on zero-shot object navigation benchmarks demonstrate that MVP-Nav significantly outperforms existing depth-free methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and validating that structured physical priors can effectively compensate for the absence of active depth sensors.

</details>


### [99] [LeCropFollow: Latent Space Planning for Navigation in Unstructured Crop Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31941)
*Felipe Tommaselli,Francisco Affonso,Arthur Pompeu,Gianluca Capezzuto,Arun Narenthiran Sivakumar,Girish Chowdhary,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeCropFollow提出了一种基于潜在表征的视觉导航框架，通过自监督语义热图提取器和MBRL规划器，绕过显式几何建模，在非结构化农业环境中实现零样本迁移，并在种植间隙中将语义故障减少2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何方法将高维视觉数据压缩为确定性空间参考，丢弃了不确定性和语义上下文，导致在非结构化导航特征（如不规则种植或间断）中频繁失败。

Method: 集成自监督语义热图提取器与TD-MPC2（基于模型的强化学习规划器），在潜在流形上直接优化轨迹，无需显式几何建模，保留热图信号的语义上下文。

Result: 在晚期玉米田实验中，LeCropFollow在非结构化行间与基线持平，在种植间隙中语义故障减少2.4倍（相比基于关键点的方法），且实现从简化仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 潜在规划是几何估计的稳健替代方案，特别适用于异构农业环境中的导航任务。

Abstract: Unstructured navigational features, such as irregular planting or discontinuities, remain the primary failure mode for under-canopy agricultural robots. Existing geometric approaches often fail in these scenarios because they compress high-dimensional visual data into deterministic spatial references, effectively discarding the uncertainty and semantic context required to navigate ambiguous terrain. To address this, we present LeCropFollow, a visual navigation framework that bypasses explicit geometric modeling in favor of a learned latent representation. By integrating a self-supervised semantic heatmap extractor with TD-MPC2, a Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) planner, our system optimizes trajectories directly within a latent manifold. The framework operates over the uncompressed heatmap signal, preserving the semantic context that geometric reductions discard. We demonstrate that this representational shift enables zero-shot transfer from simplified simulation to the physical world without fine-tuning. Extensive field experiments in late-stage corn fields show that LeCropFollow matches state-of-the-art baselines in unstructured rows but significantly outperforms them in plantation gaps, achieving a 2.4x reduction in semantic failures compared to keypoint-based methods. These results suggest that latent planning offers a robust alternative to geometric estimation for operations in heterogeneous agricultural environments. Code, models, and data available: https://felipe-tommaselli.github.io/lecropfollow .

</details>


### [100] [RRT-Rope: A deterministic shortening approach for fast near-optimal path planning in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31948)
*Louis Petit,Alexis Lussier Desbiens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RRT-Rope算法，通过改进的RRT-Connect快速生成可行路径，再结合确定性捷径技术进行后处理，在大型无遮挡3D环境（如地下矿山采场）中实现比现有RRT变体快70%的规划速度，同时保持近最优路径质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法在大型无遮挡3D环境（如地下矿山采场）中计算成本高、收敛时间长，亟需一种能快速找到近最优解的高效算法。

Method: RRT-Rope：先用改进版RRT-Connect快速生成可行路径，再利用确定性捷径技术对路径进行后处理优化，通过利用树分支中插入的中间节点加速捷径过程。

Result: 在仿真环境中，RRT-Rope在路径成本和计算时间上均优于其他流行RRT变体和缩短技术，在代表性采场中比次优算法快70%。

Conclusion: RRT-Rope通过快速可行路径生成加确定性后处理捷径的组合策略，在大型3D环境中实现了显著的性能提升，为无人机在复杂工业环境中的实时路径规划提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Many path planning algorithms have been introduced so far, but most are costly, in path cost and in processing time, in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments such as underground mining stopes explored by an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) algorithms are popular because of their probabilistic completeness and rapidity in finding a feasible path in single-query problems. Many of the algorithms (e.g. Informed RRT*, RRT#) developed to improve RRT need considerable time to converge in large environments. Shortcutting an RRT is an old idea that has been proven to outperform RRT variants. This paper introduces a new method, RRT-Rope, that aims at finding a near-optimal solution in a drastically shorter amount of time. The proposed approach benefits from fast computation of a feasible path with an altered version of RRT-connect, and post-processes it quickly with a deterministic shortcutting technique, taking advantage of intermediate nodes added to each branch of the tree. This paper presents simulations and statistics carried out to show the efficiency of RRT-Rope, which gives better results in terms of path cost and computation time than other popular RRT variations and shortening techniques in all our simulation environments, and is up to 70% faster than the next best algorithm in a representative stope.

</details>


### [101] [Adapting Generalist Robot Policies with Semantic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31958)
*Jagdeep Singh Bhatia,Andrew Wagenmaker,William Chen,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出语义动作强化学习（SARL），通过优化语言提示空间而非直接优化机器人动作，利用通用策略模型中的预训练技能，在复杂长期任务上实现高效在线强化学习，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略虽能从大规模预训练中获得丰富技能，但标准RL方法因需要策略动作分布与高效策略分布接近，在分布外的复杂长期任务上难以直接适配。

Method: 提出SARL框架，将通用策略作为可控技能先验，在语言提示空间中通过在线交互进行强化学习，利用预训练技能而非从头学习新技能，从而产生结构化且有语义意义的探索。

Result: 在真实场景和模拟基准测试中，SARL解锁了全新能力——适应VLA行为以解决复杂长期任务，并显著优于现有提升机器人部署行为的方法。

Conclusion: 语言提示是通用策略中有效的优化空间，通过学习调制提示可组合已有技能解决超出零样本能力的任务，实现高效的在线策略改进。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies learn a diverse repertoire of behaviors from large-scale pretraining. In principle, this makes them excellent priors for downstream adaptation via reinforcement learning (RL). In practice, however, standard RL methods leveraging this prior optimize directly over robot actions, requiring the base policy's action distribution to be close to that of a performant policy from the start. This assumption breaks down for complex or long-horizon tasks that fall outside the pretraining distribution. Our key insight is that, for sufficiently expressive generalist policies, language prompts are an effective alternative space for learning to solve such tasks: modulating language inputs elicits skills already within the policy's repertoire, which can be composed to solve tasks beyond its zero-shot capabilities. We propose Semantic Action Reinforcement Learning (SARL), which learns to optimize this prompt space through online interaction, treating the generalist policy as a controllable skill prior. Importantly, leveraging pretrained skills rather than learning new ones from scratch yields structured, semantically meaningful exploration and highly efficient online improvement, and learning to modulate prompts through experience grounds them in induced real-world behaviors for robust task-solving. Across real-world settings and simulated benchmarks, we show SARL unlocks fundamentally new capabilities -- adapting VLA behavior to solve complex, long-horizon tasks -- and significantly outperforms existing approaches for improving robot behavior in deployment.

</details>


### [102] [OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31993)
*Arnav Balaji,Arpit Bahety,Sriniket Ambatipudi,Daniel Lam,Junhong Xu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OOPSIEVERSE是一个统一的仿真框架和基准测试，用于检测、量化和表示家庭机器人操作中的物理损伤（机械、热、流体），支持在多种仿真器中实现损伤感知，并通过多个用例验证其在安全策略学习、安全评估和仿真到现实迁移中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作仿真器缺乏通用的机制来检测、量化和表示损伤，使得机器人在执行任务时可能对自身或环境造成物理伤害，阻碍了家庭机器人的安全部署。

Method: 提出OOPSIEVERSE框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) DAMAGESIM——一个与仿真器无关的损伤检测与量化框架，将接触力、温度变化、液体交互等信号转化为机械、热或流体损伤；(2) 一套家庭任务基准，用于评估常见损伤模式并区分任务完成与安全执行。在OmniGibson（Nvidia Omniverse）和RoboCasa（MuJoCo）两个不同物理引擎的仿真器上实例化DAMAGESIM。

Result: 通过四个用例展示了框架的实用性：(1) 通过实时损伤反馈引导更安全的示范收集；(2) 通过损伤条件模仿学习和强化学习学习更安全的操作策略；(3) 对现有视觉-语言-动作策略进行安全基准测试；(4) 提升仿真到现实迁移策略的真实世界安全性。

Conclusion: OOPSIEVERSE作为开源基础框架，为系统化、可扩展的安全机器人操作研究提供了统一平台，能够显式地将损伤作为物理可量化的信号融入仿真训练与评估中。

Abstract: While robotic manipulation capabilities have advanced rapidly, physical safety remains a major barrier to deploying household robots: task success is insufficient if the robot damages itself or its surroundings. Simulation offers a harm-free alternative to costly and dangerous real-world training and evaluation, yet existing simulators lack general mechanisms to detect, quantify, and represent damage. To address this gap, we introduce OOPSIEVERSE, a unified simulation framework and benchmark for damage-aware household manipulation. OOPSIEVERSE provides damage as an explicit, physically-grounded, and taskagnostic signal by converting sources such as contact forces, temperature changes, and liquid interactions into corresponding mechanical, thermal or fluid damage. OOPSIEVERSE comprises two core elements: (1) DAMAGESIM, a simulator-agnostic framework for detecting and quantifying damage during navigation and manipulation, and (2) a suite of household tasks designed to evaluate common damage modes and distinguish between task completion and safe execution. We demonstrate the generality of our framework by instantiating DAMAGESIM in two simulators with different physics backends, OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) and RoboCasa (MuJoCo). We further showcase the utility of OOPSIEVERSE across multiple use cases, including (1) guiding safer demonstration collection via real-time damage feedback, (2) learning safer manipulation policies through damage-conditioned imitation learning and reinforcement learning, (3) benchmarking the safety of state-of-the-art Vision Language Action policies, and (4) improving real-world safety of sim-to-real transferred policies. Together, our results highlight the potential of OOPSIEVERSE as an open-source foundation for systematic, scalable research on safe robot manipulation. For code and more information, please refer to https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/

</details>


### [103] [Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32009)
*Xiaopeng Lin,Ruoqi Yang,Shijie Lian,Zhaolong Shen,Bin Yu,Changti Wu,Haibao Liu,Yuxiang Zhang,Hong Li,Qiyuan Su,Haochen Liu,Xuguo He,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Zhirui Zhang,Bojun Cheng,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Human-as-Humanoid框架，通过同步ego-exo视频、运动重定向、分段逆运动学和正运动学感知监督，将人类示范数据转化为高自由度人形机器人可执行的观测-动作监督，实现4.8-7.2倍于遥操作的数据采集效率提升，并支持零目标任务机器人演示的下游策略泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖于高质量的观测-动作监督数据，但为高自由度人形机器人规模化采集此类数据极为困难。遥操作虽能提供控制器对齐的监督，但速度慢、成本高；人类日常视频虽丰富但不能直接提供机器人可执行的动作。需要一种将人类示范转化为机器人可用的观测-动作监督框架。

Method: 基于60-DoF上半身人形机器人PrimeU，使用同步ego-exo视频配对部署对齐的自我中心观测与外部运动恢复；通过分段逆运动学将恢复的人类运动重定向为控制器对齐的60-DoF动作块；利用正运动学感知监督训练VLA模型以保持手腕和指尖的任务空间几何结构。

Result: 在数据采集分析中实现4.8-7.2倍于遥操作的原始示范吞吐量增益；在多个下游任务中，仅使用转换后的人类标签进行后训练的策略无需目标任务机器人演示即可泛化到真实机器人部署。

Conclusion: Human-as-Humanoid有效将大规模人类视觉示范转化为可执行的观测-动作监督，显著提升数据采集效率并支持零演示泛化，为高自由度人形机器人的VLA训练提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models across robot embodiments require high-quality observation--action supervision to learn deployable action distributions, yet scaling such robot data remains difficult, especially for high-DoF humanoids. Teleoperation provides controller-aligned supervision, while human egocentric videos capture diverse bimanual manipulation but do not directly provide executable robot actions. We introduce Human-as-Humanoid, a human-to-humanoid supervision framework that enables near-real-time human-centric action generation, making human demonstrations usable for high-DoF humanoid VLA training by jointly aligning the robot embodiment, the sensing setup, and the action-label interface. Built on PrimeU, a human-aligned 60-DoF upper-body humanoid, Human-as-Humanoid uses synchronized ego-exo videos to pair deployment-aligned egocentric observations with exocentric motion recovery, retargets the recovered human motion through staged Inverse Kinematics (IK) into controller-aligned 60-DoF action chunks, and trains the VLA model with Forward Kinematics (FK)-aware supervision to preserve wrist and fingertip task-space geometry. This converts large-scale human demonstrations from visual observations into executable observation--action supervision for the target humanoid. Experiments validate the conversion chain at the motion-recovery, robot-action-space, and real-robot deployment levels. Human-as-Humanoid yields a 4.8--7.2x raw demonstration-throughput gain over humanoid teleoperation in our data-collection analysis, and on several downstream tasks, policies post-trained only with the converted human labels generalize to real-robot deployment without target-task robot demonstrations. The official project website is available at https://zgc-embodyai.github.io/Human-as-Humanoid.

</details>


### [104] [Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32027)
*Marcel Torne,Anubha Mahajan,Abhijnya Bhat,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FPL是一种从自由形式的人类偏好中学习机器人策略的方法，允许标注员定义自然语言偏好轴（如速度、安全性等）并沿每个轴提供成对偏好，从而学习语言条件奖励模型以训练奖励条件策略。在四个真实世界和两个模拟长时域操作任务中，FPL相比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升了38个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励设计是自主机器人策略改进的核心瓶颈，尤其在长时域操作任务中，稀疏的成功标签提供信号过少，而二元偏好将许多竞争性的质量概念压缩成一个模糊的信号。

Method: FPL允许标注员定义自然语言偏好轴（如速度、安全性、放置质量、细心程度），并沿每个轴提供成对偏好；基于这些标注学习语言条件奖励模型，将轨迹和偏好标签映射到轴特定奖励；再使用该模型训练奖励条件策略，以优化多个人类指定的维度。

Result: 在四个真实世界和两个模拟长时域操作任务中，FPL比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升38个百分点；学习到密集的进度信号而无需显式子任务分割；展现出数据中不存在的行为组合性；允许用户在测试时引导策略走向不同行为而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: FPL通过自由形式自然语言偏好轴有效解决了长时域操作任务中奖励设计的瓶颈问题，显著优于传统方法，并具备组合性和可操控性等额外优势。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck for autonomous robot policy improvement, especially in long-horizon manipulation tasks where sparse success labels provide too little signal and binary preferences collapse many competing notions of quality into one ambiguous signal. We introduce Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), a method for learning robot policies from freeform human preferences. Rather than asking annotators which of two trajectories is better overall, FPL lets them define natural-language preference axes, such as speed, safety, quality of placement, or carefulness, and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations are used to learn a language-conditioned reward model that maps a trajectory and preference label to an axis-specific reward. We use this model to train a reward-conditioned policy that optimizes across the multiple human-specified dimensions. Across four real-world and two simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, FPL improves over sparse-reward and binary-preference methods by 38 percentage points. Beyond improved performance, FPL learns dense progress signals without explicit subtask segmentation, shows compositionality of behavior not present in the data, and allows users to steer the policy towards different behaviors at test time without retraining. Blog post with videos available at https://freeform-pl.github.io/fpl.website/

</details>


### [105] [DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32028)
*Ziyu Shan,Zhenyu Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DVG-WM，一种将世界模型解耦为动力学学习和视觉合成的高效框架，通过级联机制实现快速、高保真的机器人操作视频预测，在LIBERO和真实平台上实现最高3.97倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的具身世界模型存在根本性纠缠：准确建模动力学需要低层次时序推理，而生成高分辨率帧需要高层次语义驱动的视觉合成，导致推理速度慢或预测过于粗糙，难以保留接触细节。

Method: 提出解耦视频生成世界模型(DVG-WM)：1) 将世界模型显式分解为动力学学习和视觉合成两个独立模块；2) 基于初始观测和语言指令首先生成中间视觉状态预览物理交互；3) 提出高效级联机制，使用流匹配将动力学直接映射到视频潜在空间；4) 引入潜在退化机制以再生接触丰富细节。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实机器人平台上验证，视频质量提升的同时实现最高3.97倍加速，表明解耦视频生成可作为高效的具身世界模型用于机器人操作。

Conclusion: 解耦视频生成策略有效解决了世界模型中动力学建模与视觉合成之间的冲突，为机器人操作提供了兼顾效率与保真度的新范式。

Abstract: Video-based embodied world models provide an appealing substrate for robotic manipulation by predicting future states, yet current approaches remain limited by a fundamental entanglement: accurately modeling dynamics typically requires low-level temporal reasoning, while producing high-resolution frames demands expansive visual synthesis according to high-level semantics. This entanglement results in slow inference speed for iterative planning or too coarse predictions to retain contact-rich details. To solve this dilemma, we present Disentangled Video Generation World Model (DVG-WM), an efficient framework that explicitly decomposes world modeling into dynamics learning and visual synthesis. Conditioned on an initial observation and a language instruction, our model first generates a plausible sequence of intermediate visual states to preview the physical interaction and refines them to obtain high-fidelity videos. Furthermore, an efficient cascading mechanism is proposed, where DVG-WM uses flow matching to directly map the dynamics to video latents, and introduces a latent degradation mechanism to regenerate contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms demonstrate improved video quality with up to 3.97 times acceleration, validating that disentangled video generation can be an efficient embodied world model for robotic manipulation.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Unveiling the Multiphysics Complexity: An Isogeometric Framework for Inducing Bifurcation and Tracing Post-Buckling Paths in Electroelastic Thin Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30674)
*Zhaowei Liu,Long Jin,Andrew McBride,Weicheng Huang,Tiantang Yu,Peiliang Bian,Paul Steinmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种等几何Kirchhoff-Love壳公式，用于电弹性薄结构的非线性分析，能够准确捕捉大变形、对称性破缺失稳和屈曲后响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弹性壳在软致动器、传感器和能量采集器中应用广泛，但其复杂的非线性多物理场耦合（包括分叉和屈曲后响应）的精确模拟仍具有挑战性。

Method: 采用等几何Kirchhoff-Love壳公式，整合几何非线性运动学、Maxwell应力诱导的机电耦合、材料不可压缩性和初始预拉伸；使用Catmull-Clark细分曲面确保C1连续性；解析推导一致切线算子；引入静态凝聚过程满足平面应力约束；采用带弧长连续和特征模态扰动的分阶段Newton-Raphson算法追踪分叉和屈曲后平衡路径。

Result: 通过球膜、预拉伸圆板和环形膜等数值算例，验证了该框架在机电耦合加载下准确捕捉大变形、对称性破缺失稳和屈曲后响应的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的等几何框架有效解决了电弹性薄壳结构非线性多物理场耦合模拟中的关键挑战，为软电弹性结构的分析和设计提供了可靠的计算工具。

Abstract: Electroelastic shells are widely used in soft actuators, sensors, and energy harvesters owing to their large electrically induced deformations. However, the accurate simulation of their complex nonlinear multiphysics coupling, including bifurcation and post-buckling responses, remains challenging. This work presents an isogeometric Kirchhoff-Love shell formulation for the nonlinear analysis of electroelastic thin structures undergoing finite deformations. The formulation incorporates geometrically nonlinear kinematics, Maxwell-stress-induced electromechanical coupling, material incompressibility, and initial prestretch. Catmull--Clark subdivision surfaces are employed to ensure the C1 continuity required by Kirchhoff--Love shell theory. Consistent tangent operators are derived analytically, and a static condensation procedure is introduced to satisfy the plane-stress constraint. To trace bifurcation and post-buckling equilibrium paths, a staged Newton--Raphson algorithm with arc-length continuation and eigenmode perturbation is adopted. Numerical examples involving spherical membranes, prestretched circular plates, and toroidal membranes demonstrate the capability of the proposed framework to accurately capture large deformations, symmetry-breaking instabilities, and post-buckling responses under coupled electromechanical loading.

</details>


### [107] [Stochastic Analysis of Fade Duration Using Wiener Chaos Expansion and Malliavin Calculus: Optimal Importance Sampling via Adaptive SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30692)
*Francisco Delgado-Vences*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Wiener混沌展开、Malliavin微积分和自适应重要性采样的理论框架，用于高精度分析无线信道衰落持续时间分布，在概率低至10⁻¹⁵的尾事件下实现相对误差<0.5%，方差缩减达839-2516倍，且无需O(1/P)量级样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Rice电平穿越理论和蒙特卡洛模拟在分析信道衰落持续时间的尾事件时精度不足且计算开销巨大，需要一种高精度、计算高效的稀有事件概率估计方法。

Method: (i) 使用Wiener混沌展开最小化蒙特卡洛方差进行矩估计和CCDF表征；(ii) 通过Markovian投影将无穷维动态降维至≤3维系统；(iii) 基于Malliavin灵敏度推导渐进最优重要性采样权重；(iv) 采用Robbins-Monro步长调度的自适应SGD算法进行参数估计。

Result: 数值验证相对误差低于0.5%，方差缩减达到839-2516倍，能在P~10⁻¹⁵的极低概率下进行梯度优化，无需O(1/P)量级的样本。

Conclusion: 该框架为衰落持续时间统计提供了一种理论上严谨、计算高效的解决方案，通过解析Malliavin权重评估灵敏度，使得极低概率事件的梯度优化成为可能。

Abstract: Characterizing fade duration in wireless channels is fundamental for designing robust communication systems. Classical approaches -- Rice's level-crossing theory and Monte Carlo simulation -- lack precision for tail events and are computationally prohibitive for rare-event probability estimation. This paper introduces a rigorous framework combining Wiener Chaos Expansion (WCE), Malliavin Calculus, and importance sampling with adaptive weights to analyze fade duration $Z(T)$ distributions.
  Main contributions include: (i) high-accuracy moment estimation and CCDF characterization via WCE minimizing Monte Carlo variance; (ii) Markovian projection reducing infinite-dimensional dynamics to tractable systems ($\dim \leq 3$) for Rayleigh, Rician, and Nakagami models under stated assumptions; (iii) asymptotically optimal importance sampling weights derived from Malliavin sensitivities, achieving 839 to 2516x variance reductions; (iv) a theoretically grounded and provably efficient adaptive SGD algorithm with Robbins-Monro step size schedule for parameter estimation. Numerical experiments validate our approach with relative errors below 0.5\%, enabling gradient-based optimization of fade duration statistics even for regimes where $P \sim 10^{-15}$, without requiring $\mathcal{O}(1/P)$ samples, by evaluating sensitivities through analytical Malliavin weights.

</details>


### [108] [Vector alignment in matrix Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30868)
*Congzhou M Sha*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种李代数方法，用于从含噪声的配对观测数据中恢复任意经典矩阵李群（GL(n), SL(n), SO(n), U(n), SO(p,q), Sp(n), Spin(n), SE(n)）的群元素，该方法通过伪逆、矩阵对数、投影到李代数和矩阵指数四步实现无噪声情况下的精确恢复，并引入了牛顿风格修正以改善噪声情况下的最优性。所有投影及其最优性已在Lean 4中形式化证明，并在Julia中进行了数值实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限且含噪声的配对观测数据中恢复观测者之间的规范差异（即群元素），在理论和实验中都有应用价值。现有方法如Kabsch和Horn算法仅适用于SO(3)，本文旨在将李代数方法推广到所有经典矩阵李群。

Method: 方法包含四个步骤：(1) 伪逆；(2) 矩阵对数；(3) 投影到李代数——唯一依赖群类型的步骤，产生最小二乘最优的李代数元素；(4) 矩阵指数。对于噪声数据，进一步引入牛顿风格修正，在无噪声情况下匹配李代数方法，在有噪声情况下介于李代数方法与直接最小二乘优化之间。所有投影及修正的数学基础在Lean 4.31.0中形式化证明。

Result: 给出了针对经典矩阵李群（GL(n), SL(n), SO(n), U(n), SO(p,q), Sp(n), Spin(n), SE(n)）的显式投影公式。投影产生的李代数元素满足：像位于李代数中且残差与李代数正交。牛顿修正的性能介于未修正的李代数方法和直接最小二乘优化之间。所有证明在Lean中通过形式化验证，数值实验在Julia中完成。

Conclusion: 李代数方法成功从含噪声数据中恢复经典矩阵李群的群元素，提供了统一的显式公式框架。投影步骤是唯一依赖群类型的关键步骤，且具有最小二乘最优性。牛顿修正进一步提升了噪声数据的处理性能。形式化证明确保了方法的数学严谨性。

Abstract: The difference in gauge between two observers of the same physical system can be thought of as a group element acting on their common vector representations. Recovering that group element from a finite, noisy list of paired observations may be of use in both theory and experiment. The Kabsch and Horn algorithms efficiently align point clouds in $\mathbb R^3$, reconciling rotated frames of reference in Galilean relativity (i.e. $SO(3)$). In a previous work, we proposed an alternative Lie algebra method which extends to the Lorentz group $SO(3,1)_+$, and putatively to all Lie groups. In this work, we report the explicit formulae for applying the Lie algebra method to the classical matrix Lie groups (general linear $GL(n)$, special linear $SL(n)$, special orthogonal $SO(n)$, unitary $U(n)$, indefinite special orthogonal $SO(p,q)$, symplectic $Sp(n)$, spin $Spin(n)$, special Euclidean $SE(n)$) over both the real and complex fields. The four steps (pseudoinverse, matrix logarithm, projection onto the Lie algebra, matrix exponential) are exact in the noiseless case. The only group-dependent step is the projection, which we show produces the unique least squares-optimal element of the Lie algebra whenever its image lies in $\mathfrak g$ and its residual is orthogonal to $\mathfrak g$. Additionally, the Lie algebra method is optimal only to leading order for noisy data, so we refine it with a Newton-style correction. This correction matches the Lie algebra method in the noiseless case and direct least squares optimization in the noisy case, with performance between that of the Lie algebra method without correction and naive least squares optimization. The projections, their optimality, and the identity underlying the correction are formally proven in Lean~4.31.0 (with Mathlib 4.31.0), and numerical experiments are benchmarked in Julia.

</details>


### [109] [Local Gevrey regularity and Quasi-Monte Carlo quadrature for PDEs parameterized on non-compact domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30972)
*Dinh Dũng,Tùng Lê,Alexey Chernov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了参数化散度型扩散椭圆偏微分方程弱解的局部Gevrey正则性，并基于此发展了无穷维积分的维度截断误差理论，证明了拟蒙特卡洛求积的收敛率，结合有限元离散误差给出了完全离散逼近的完整误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对参数化扩散椭圆PDE弱解建立局部Gevrey正则性理论，并基于此框架发展无穷维积分中维度截断误差的理论处理，为完全离散逼近提供完整的误差分析。

Method: 1) 在非紧致参数域上假设扩散系数具有局部Gevrey正则性，证明弱解在H^1范数下参数导数的显式界；2) 基于局部Gevrey框架发展维度截断误差的理论处理；3) 证明具有局部Gevrey正则性的被积函数在高斯加权积分下拟蒙特卡洛求积的收敛率；4) 结合有限元离散误差、维度截断误差和拟蒙特卡洛求积误差，给出完全离散逼近的完整误差分析。

Result: 建立了弱解的局部Gevrey正则性并给出了参数导数的显式H^1界，证明了QMC求积的收敛率，数值实验验证了理论收敛率。

Conclusion: 局部Gevrey正则性框架成功用于维度截断误差的理论处理，结合有限元离散和QMC求积给出了完全离散逼近的收敛率分析。

Abstract: We establish local Gevrey regularity for the weak solution to parametric divergence-form diffusion elliptic PDEs, assuming the diffusion coefficient itself possesses local Gevrey parametric regularity over a non-compact domain. Here "local Gevrey regularity" means that the regularity is determined in a neighborhood of each parametric point and depends on that point. Explicit bounds on parametric derivatives of the weak solution are proved in the $H^1$-norm. Building on this local Gevrey framework, we develop a novel theoretical treatment of the dimension truncation error for infinite-dimensional integration. We prove convergence rates of quasi-Monte Carlo quadrature for the finite-dimensional Gaussian-weighted integration of integrands having local Gevrey regularity. Together with the finite element discretization error, the errors arising from dimension truncation and quasi-Monte Carlo quadrature yield a complete error analysis and convergence rates for the fully discrete approximation of a bounded linear functional of the weak solution. Numerical experiments confirming the theoretical convergence rates are presented.

</details>


### [110] [Ricci-Notation Tensor Framework for Numerical Algebraic Geometry via Any-Degree Unitary-Triangular Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31003)
*Dileepan Joseph*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了任意次数的酉三角（Qr）分解，将线性代数中的QR分解推广到多项式系统。通过里奇符号张量框架，系数张量分解为酉因子和三角因子的乘积，可三角化多项式系统并保持解集不变。在零维系统上的数值实验与符号化的Gröbner基方法进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性代数中的QR分解可稳健高效地求解线性系统，但缺乏类似方法求解高次多项式系统。本文旨在提出一种可与QR分解相媲美的、适用于任意次多项式系统的数值方法。

Method: 提出任意次数的酉三角（Qr）分解，基于里奇符号张量框架（协同设计的代数与软件）。多项式系数由向量形稀疏张量表示，其张量次数等于最高单项式次数。Qr分解将系数张量分解为酉因子和三角因子（均含里奇符号指标和稀疏项），酉因子定义了张量代数中的酉变换，可在保持解集（零维或正维）的同时三角化多项式系统。

Result: 将方法应用于两个零维多项式系统（一个3次3方程3未知数，一个2次4方程4未知数），并与Gröbner基方法的符号三角化结果进行了比较。

Conclusion: 所提Qr分解在形式上类似于Gröbner基三角化，但其理论根源完全不同，属于数值方法范畴，为多项式系统的数值求解提供了新的途径。

Abstract: The unitary-triangular (QR) factorization of linear algebra may be used to robustly and efficiently solve a linear system. Toward a comparable numerical method to solve a polynomial system of higher degree, this paper proposes an any-degree unitary-triangular (Qr) factorization, which for a degree-one system reduces to the QR factorization. The work develops a tensor framework, i.e., codesigned algebra and software, where polynomial system coefficients are represented by a vector-shaped sparse tensor, a multidimensional array whose number of Ricci-notation indices, called the tensor degree, equals the highest monomial degree of the system. With the proposed Qr factorization, the coefficient tensor decomposes into a product of unitary and triangular factors that, in general, also have Ricci-notation indices and sparse entries. The unitary factor defines a unitary transform, a generalization of the related linear algebra concept to tensor algebra, that can triangularize a polynomial system while preserving its solution set, whether zero- or positive-dimensional. The work extends the author's Ricci-notation tensor framework, providing new algebra and new software to model, construct, and factorize polynomial systems in this manner. After applying the approach to numerically triangularize two zero-dimensional systems, chosen for educational value, results are compared to the Gröbner-basis (GB) method for triangularizing polynomial systems symbolically. One problem is of degree three, with three equations and unknowns, and the other of degree two, with four equations and unknowns. Although it resembles GB triangularization, the proposed Qr factorization has a completely different pedigree associated with numerical methods.

</details>


### [111] [Online TT-ALS for Streaming Tensor Decomposition with Incremental Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31061)
*Hiroki Takeda,Yuto Miyatake,Daisuke Furihata*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出Online TT-ALS算法，通过顺序施加正交约束实现张量序列的高效精确更新，在保持高重构精度的同时将计算复杂度从二次降至线性（O(I^{n-1}r)），并在视频质量指标上优于现有在线方法和深度学习方法，适合低延迟实时处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式数据下的张量列分解方法中，批处理方法虽重构精度高但内存消耗大，在线方法计算效率高但精度不足，缺乏兼顾精度与效率的方案。

Method: 提出Online TT-ALS算法，通过顺序施加正交规范约束，实现核心张量的精确更新；采用确定性单次扫描更新策略，将秩依赖从二次降为线性。

Result: 理论证明了正交约束保证了局部目标函数单调下降和时间平滑性；实验表明该方法在数学近似精度和人眼感知视频质量上优于现有在线方法，且相比深度学习方法加速数个数量级。

Conclusion: Online TT-ALS在计算效率和重构精度之间取得了良好平衡，适用于低延迟实时流式数据处理场景。

Abstract: Tensor Train (TT) decomposition is a powerful technique for analyzing high-dimensional data. Existing algorithms for computing TT decompositions can be categorized into two main types: conventional batch-based approaches and recursive online methods. In the context of streaming data, batch methods typically achieve higher reconstruction accuracy but often suffer from memory exhaustion, while online methods provide greater computational efficiency. In this work, we introduce Online TT-ALS (Alternating Least Squares), an algorithm that sequentially enforces orthogonality constraints. This approach allows for efficient and exact updates of the core tensor while maintaining high reconstruction accuracy. Theoretically, we prove that enforcing these orthogonal gauge constraints guarantees monotonic decrease of the local objective function and temporal smoothness. Computationally, our deterministic single-sweep update reduces the rank dependence from quadratic to linear, achieving an overall complexity of $\mathcal{O}(I^{n-1} r)$. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing online techniques not only in terms of mathematical approximation accuracy but also in human perception-based video quality metrics. Furthermore, compared to recent deep learning-based paradigms, our algebraic approach achieves speedups of several orders of magnitude. Consequently, our method exhibits high computational efficiency and is suitable for low-latency real-time processing applications.

</details>


### [112] [A consistent-splitting generalized scalar auxiliary variable scheme for the perturbed Boussinesq system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31152)
*M Nader Alhomsi,Jiahong Wu,Xiaoming Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种基于广义标量辅助变量（GSAV）的二阶一致分裂格式，用于二维扰动Boussinesq系统。该格式将非线性对流和线性浮力-分层耦合项显式处理，每时间步只需解耦求解少量线性系统。证明了无条件弱稳定性定理，并导出了速度、压力和温度的最优二阶误差估计，但误差常数依赖于逆粘性和逆热扩散系数的四重嵌套指数，因此当两者趋于零时格式不稳健。数值实验验证了二阶收敛性，并复现了长期分层流模拟中预期的内波动力学和指数松弛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对标准Boussinesq系统减去稳定线性分层静力平衡后得到的扰动Boussinesq系统，现有数值方法在保持能量稳定性和高阶精度的同时处理非线性对流与线性耦合项的困难，需要发展一种高效、稳定且具有严格误差分析的数值格式。

Method: 基于GSAV方法，扩展Huang和Shen[17]的Navier-Stokes方程一致分裂广义BDF2框架，将非线性对流、平流项与线性浮力、分层耦合项均显式处理，使每时间步简化为少数解耦线性系统。

Result: 证明了GSAV格式的无条件弱稳定性定理；推导了速度、压力和温度的最优二阶误差估计；揭示了误差常数依赖于逆粘性和逆热扩散系数的四重嵌套指数；数值实验确认了二阶收敛性，并成功复现内波动力学和指数松弛行为。

Conclusion: 所提GSAV格式在二阶精度和解耦效率方面表现优异，但存在粘性和热扩散系数趋于零时格式不稳健的局限性，为后续改进格式的鲁棒性提供了理论依据和方向。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a second-order consistent-splitting scheme, based on the generalized scalar auxiliary variable (GSAV) approach, for the two-dimensional perturbed Boussinesq system. The system is obtained by subtracting a stable, linearly stratified hydrostatic equilibrium from the standard Boussinesq system. The time discretization extends the consistent-splitting generalized BDF2 framework of Huang and Shen [17] for the Navier-Stokes equations, treating the nonlinear convection and advection together with the linear buoyancy and stratification couplings explicitly, so that each time step reduces to a small number of decoupled linear systems. We prove an unconditional weak stability theorem for the GSAV scheme and derive optimal second-order error estimates for the velocity, pressure, and temperature. A careful tracing reveals that the error constant depends on the inverse viscosity and inverse thermal diffusivity through a quadruply-nested exponential, so the scheme is not robust as either tends to zero. Numerical experiments confirm the second-order convergence and reproduce the expected internal-wave dynamics and exponential relaxation toward hydrostatic balance in a long-time stratified-flow simulation.

</details>


### [113] [GKB Methods for X-Ray Computed Tomography with an Unmatched Back Projector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31153)
*Abdulmajeed Alsubhi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对大规模X射线CT反问题中因前后投影器离散化不匹配导致共轭梯度法失效的问题，提出AB和BA GKB算法作为GKB的预处理形式，有效解决不匹配算子对问题，在数值实验中表现出更强的抗噪稳定性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT反问题中前向投影器和反向投影器常采用不同离散化方式，导致算子对不匹配，进而使正规方程不一致，传统共轭梯度法无法得到有用解，需要开发适用于不匹配算子对的高效求解算法。

Method: 提出AB和BA GKB算法，作为Golub-Kahn双对角化方法的预处理形式，将原始大规模问题投影到低维子空间，通过稀疏下双对角矩阵的奇异值分解求解；讨论了浮点运算次数层面的理论计算成本。

Result: 算法对不匹配算子对问题有效，实现简单且可调参；相比许多Krylov方法存在的半收敛（噪声放大）问题，所提算法表现出更强的抗噪稳定性；多种CT问题的数值实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: AB和BA GKB算法是解决CT中不匹配算子对问题的有效预处理策略，能够提供更稳定的解，优于传统Krylov方法。

Abstract: In large scale X ray Computed Tomography (CT) inverse problems, the forward and back projectors are often generated using different discretizations. This discrepancy leads to unmatched pairs of projections, resulting in inconsistent normal equations. Consequently, employing the Conjugate Gradient method does not produce a useful solution. For matched operator pairs, the Golub Kahan bidiagonalization (GKB) method provides an efficient solution strategy. It works by projecting the original large-scale problem onto a lower-dimensional subspace, enabling the solution to be computed via a singular value decomposition of a sparse lower bidiagonal matrix. To address unmatched-pair problems in CT, we propose the AB and BA GKB algorithms as preconditioned forms of the GKB. These methods are straightforward to implement and allow for parameter tuning. We provide a discussion on the theoretical computational costs of our proposed algorithms in terms of floating point operations and compare with existing methods. While many Krylov methods tend to amplify noise in solutions, leading to semiconvergence, our proposed algorithms demonstrate greater resilience against this effect. We validate the effectiveness of our approach through numerical examples across various CT problems, showcasing its ability to deliver more stable solutions.

</details>


### [114] [Higher-order exponential Runge-Kutta Galerkin finite element method for semilinear parabolic problems with nonsmooth data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31231)
*Shuo Yang,Runjie Zhang,Zhe Yu,Jinwei Fang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑初值半线性抛物问题，建立了线性Galerkin有限元空间离散与高阶显式指数Runge-Kutta时间离散耦合格式的严格数值分析框架，证明了p阶EERK方法的收敛阶为min(1+γ/2+ρ₁(γ)/2, p)，数值实验验证了估计的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑初值情形下，非线性项和精确解的高阶导数缺乏先验估计，传统的光滑误差分析方法不再适用，亟需建立适用于该情况的严格数值分析框架。

Method: 结合解析半群技术和分数幂空间理论，对非线性项和精确解的高阶导数建立严格有界性估计，进而推导收敛阶。

Result: p阶EERK方法的收敛阶为min(1+γ/2+ρ₁(γ)/2, p)，其中γ刻画初值正则性，ρ₁(γ)量化非线性项一阶Fréchet导数的有界性。

Conclusion: 成功建立了非光滑初值半线性抛物问题的高阶显式指数Runge-Kutta方法的收敛性理论，数值实验证实了理论估计的锐利性。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous numerical analysis framework for a class of semilinear parabolic problems with nonsmooth initial data. We employ a linear Galerkin finite element method for spatial discretization coupled with a high-order explicit exponential Runge-Kutta (EERK) temporal integration scheme. In contrast to conventional smooth error analysis, the nonsmooth case lacks a priori estimates for the higher-order derivatives of both the nonlinear term and the exact solution. By combining analytic semigroup techniques with fractional power space theory, we establish rigorous bounds for these derivatives. Finally, our analysis proves that the $p$th-order EERK method achieves a convergence rate of $\min(1 + γ/2 + ρ_1(γ)/2,\:p)$, where $γ$ characterizes the initial data regularity and $ρ_1(γ)$ quantifies the boundedness of the nonlinearity's first Fréchet derivative. Numerical experiments confirm the sharpness of these estimates.

</details>


### [115] [Domain-Decomposed Randomized Neural Networks for Partial Differential Equations in Unbounded Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31342)
*Haixin Wang,Haoning Dang,Fei Wang,Shimin Guo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对无界域上的偏微分方程，提出了一种区域分解随机神经网络框架：近场子网络捕获局部几何特征，远场子网络表示外部衰减，通过边界和界面条件耦合，仅求解输出层系数的线性最小二乘问题。在Petrov-Galerkin和配点法两种形式下开发了具体方法，并证明了条件有界参数逼近结果和误差分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无界域上的偏微分方程难以处理：截断法需要问题依赖的人工边界条件，全局谱基对局部结构、不规则几何或近远场行为不同的解效率低下。

Method: 提出区域分解随机神经网络框架：将不同随机子网络分配至不同空间区域（近场子网络捕获局部和几何特征，远场子网络表示外部衰减），通过边界和界面条件耦合子网络，仅从Petrov-Galerkin或配点法线性最小二乘系统求解输出层系数。开发了半无界椭圆问题的Petrov-Galerkin方法和全无界、穿孔及时间依赖问题的配点法。

Result: 证明了在破碎Sobolev范数下的条件有界参数逼近结果，以及覆盖逼近误差、经验一致性/求积误差和最小二乘优化误差的误差分解。泊松方程和含时薛定谔方程的数值实验验证了方法的准确性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 所提的区域分解随机神经网络框架能够有效处理无界域PDE问题，避免了传统截断法的问题依赖边界条件，同时克服了全局谱基在局部结构和不规则几何上的局限，理论分析和数值实验均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Partial differential equations on unbounded domains are challenging because the exterior region must be represented without excessive truncation error. Truncation-based methods often require problem-dependent artificial boundary conditions, while global spectral bases may be inefficient for localized structures, irregular geometries, or solutions with different near-field and far-field behaviors. We propose a domain-decomposed randomized neural network framework for such problems. Different randomized subnetworks are assigned to different spatial regimes: a near-field subnetwork captures local and geometric features, whereas a far-field subnetwork represents exterior decay. The subnetworks are coupled by boundary and interface conditions, and only the output-layer coefficients are solved from linear least-squares systems arising from Petrov--Galerkin or collocation formulations. We develop a Petrov--Galerkin method for semi-unbounded elliptic problems and a collocation method for fully unbounded, perforated, and time-dependent problems. A conditional bounded-parameter approximation result is proved in a broken Sobolev norm, together with an error decomposition covering approximation, empirical-consistency/quadrature, and least-squares optimization errors. Numerical experiments for Poisson and time-dependent Schrödinger equations demonstrate the accuracy and flexibility of the proposed method.

</details>


### [116] [A Spectral Solver for Acoustic Scattering by Multiple Quasi-Axisymmetric Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31380)
*Jun Lai,Yuxin Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对多重准轴对称体的声散射问题，提出了一种基于Nyström离散化（结合Gauss-Legendre求积与梯形法则）的快速高精度算法，通过模态格林函数和FFT处理奇异积分，并构建了多散射体耦合求解器，实现了对多达1000个结构的有效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声散射在医学成像、地球物理勘探、声学超材料等领域应用广泛，但现有方法在处理多重准轴对称体（旋转轴为任意曲线）的散射问题时存在精度或效率瓶颈，亟需一种快速高精度算法。

Method: 基于Nyström离散化，联合Gauss-Legendre求积与梯形法则；将奇异性积分重新表述为模态格林函数及其导数的求值，利用快速傅里叶变换（FFT）和卷积高效计算；通过单体边界积分相互作用耦合构建多散射体求解器。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法可高效求解包含多达1000个准轴对称结构的多重散射问题，同时保持了良好的精度；文中还给出了光滑几何下散射问题的收敛性分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法在计算效率和精度方面均具有显著优势，适用于大规模准轴对称体声散射问题的快速求解。

Abstract: Acoustic scattering arises in a wide range of applications, including medical imaging, geophysical exploration, acoustic metamaterials, etc. In this paper, we develop a fast and highly accurate algorithm for acoustic scattering by multiple quasi-axisymmetric objects, whose axis of rotation is an arbitrary curve. The method is based on a Nyström discretization that combines Gauss-Legendre quadrature with the trapezoidal rule. To treat the singular integrals that occur when target points are close to or coincide with source points, we reformulate them as evaluations of the modal Green's function and its derivatives, which are computed efficiently using the fast Fourier transform and convolution. The multiple scattering solver is then constructed by coupling the single scatterer discretizations through inter-body boundary integral interactions. We also present a convergence analysis for scattering problems with smooth geometries. Numerical examples demonstrate the efficiency and accuracy of the proposed method for solving multiple scattering problems involving up to 1000 quasi-axisymmetric structures.

</details>


### [117] [An error analysis of discrete Kirchhoff elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31463)
*Dietmar Gallistl,Ngoc Tien Tran*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了离散基尔霍夫三角形（DKT）方法在双调和方程离散能量范数下的误差，证明了误差可由Hessian矩阵的逐常数最佳逼近和右端项振荡项界定，无需对精确解施加额外正则性假设。该结果蕴含经典DKT元素的一阶收敛性，且分析可自然推广至三维空间。导出了基于残差的后验误差估计。分析框架适用于多种经典低阶非协调元，并揭示了平面Stokes系统稳定配对与离散基尔霍夫空间之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为DKT方法提供严格的误差分析，克服传统分析中对解高正则性的依赖，并建立统一的低阶非协调元分析框架。

Method: 采用离散能量范数下的最佳逼近分析，结合Hessian矩阵的逐常数逼近和右端项振荡项进行误差界定；基于残差方法导出后验误差估计；建立通用低阶非协调元分析框架。

Result: 证明了DKT方法的一阶收敛性；导出了残差型后验误差估计；建立了与平面Stokes系统稳定配对的关系，获得正定公式和压力鲁棒误差界。

Conclusion: DKT方法的误差分析可推广至三维且无需额外正则性假设；所提通用框架适用于多种经典非协调元；DKT空间可作为Stokes系统的离散流函数空间。

Abstract: The Discrete Kirchhoff Triangle (DKT) method for the biharmonic equation is analyzed in the discrete energy norm. The error is bounded by the best approximation of the Hessian by piecewise constants and the oscillation of the right-hand side, without additional regularity assumptions on the exact solution. This result implies first-order convergence of the classical DKT element and the analysis yields a canonical extension to three space dimensions with the same approximation properties. Residual-based a posteriori error estimates are derived. The analysis is formulated within a general framework for low-order nonconforming methods, which also applies to various classical elements and yields best-approximation results by constants. It is furthermore shown how known stable pairs for the planar Stokes system have discrete stream functions in discrete Kirchhoff spaces. This yields variants of the known schemes with positive definite formulations and pressure-robust error bounds.

</details>


### [118] [V-Line Tensor Tomography in a Disk: Theoretical and Numerical Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31632)
*Rahul Bhardwaj,Madhu Gupta*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称m-张量场的V线变换，给出了核的显式刻画，推导了新的反演公式，并通过数值实验验证了向量场和对称2-张量场反演算法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称张量场的V线变换，填补该领域在核刻画和反演公式方面的理论空白，并验证相关数值算法的实际表现。

Method: 利用分解结果推导新的反演公式，对向量场和对称2-张量场进行数值实现，并在含噪场景下进行验证。

Result: 成功刻画了V线变换在对称m-张量场上的核，推导出新的反演公式，数值实验表明算法对多种模体具有有效性和鲁棒性，含噪条件下表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文的理论分析与数值验证共同表明所提方法在对称张量场V线变换反演问题中是有效且稳健的。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate V-line transforms for symmetric $m$-tensor fields whose support lies inside a disk of radius $R$ and centered at the origin. We provide an explicit characterization of the kernel of the V-line transforms acting on a symmetric $m$-tensor field and derive a new inversion formula using a decomposition result. In addition, we present a comprehensive numerical verification and validation of the inversion algorithms for these V-line transforms for vector fields and symmetric $2$-tensor fields, which were recently developed in \cite{bhardwaj_2024,bhardwaj2025tensor}. The reconstruction results obtained for various phantoms demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed numerical methods, including in the presence of noise.

</details>


### [119] [Positivity-preserving dynamical low-rank methods for the Vlasov equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31662)
*Katharina Kormann,Murtazo Nazarov,Junjie Wen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了两类保持正性的修正方法用于Vlasov方程的低秩近似，通过将正性保持等结构性质作为约束，求解二次规划问题得到最小修正项，使修正后的解在保持低秩解邻近的同时满足约束。其中一种方法还同时保持总质量和动量守恒。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩近似方法在求解高维Vlasov方程时可能出现负值等非物理解，破坏物理系统的正性等关键结构性质，因此需要引入保持正性的修正机制来确保解的物理合理性。

Method: 将正性保持等结构性质作为约束条件，通过求解二次规划问题寻找添加到低秩解上的最小修正项；提出了两种保持正性的方案，其中一种额外保持了系统的总质量和动量守恒。采用空间谱离散和时间显式Runge-Kutta格式，应用于Vlasov-Poisson和Vlasov-Poisson-BGK方程。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的两种修正方法能够有效保持低秩近似解的正性，且其中一种方法同时保持了总质量和动量守恒。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于二次规划的正性保持修正方法能有效解决低秩Vlasov求解中的非物理解问题，为保持物理系统结构性质提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we introduce positivity-preserving correction methods for low-rank approximations of the Vlasov equation. The key idea is to formulate structural properties, including positivity-preservation, as constraints and to seek a minimal correction term that is added to the low-rank solution, by solving a quadratic programming problem. As a result, the corrected solution satisfies the constraints and preserve these properties, while remaining close to the original low-rank solution. Two positivity-preserving schemes are proposed in this work, and one of them also preserves the total mass and momentum of the system. We apply the proposed methods to a Vlasov--Poisson and Vlasov--Poisson-BGK employing a spectral discretization in space and an explicit Runge--Kutta scheme in time. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [120] [Effect of different clustering approaches on the multilevel fast multipole method for the Helmholtz equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31771)
*Wolfgang Kreuzer,Christian Kasess*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了边界元法（BEM）中快速多极子方法（FMM）的聚类过程，比较了不同聚类方法对网格元素大小均匀和不均匀情况下FMM稳定性和效率的影响，并展示了错误聚类导致的数值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FMM中聚类过程对方法效率和稳定性有巨大影响，但相较于多极展开理论，聚类过程本身的研究被忽视，尤其是当网格元素大小不均匀时。

Method: 比较不同类型的聚类方法，针对均匀和非均匀元素尺寸的网格，分析其对FMM稳定性和效率的影响。

Result: 不同聚类方法对FMM-BEM的稳定性和效率有显著影响；错误聚类会导致数值问题和计算不稳定。

Conclusion: 聚类过程是FMM-BEM中不可忽视的关键环节，需针对网格特性选择合适的聚类策略以保证方法的稳定性和效率。

Abstract: The fast multipole method (FMM) is an important component for the boundary element method (BEM), because with the FMM the efficiency and feasibility of the BEM can be enhanced to a large degree. Part of the FMM is grouping the elements of the boundary element mesh into different clusters. The size of these clusters in terms of number of elements and spatial expansion has a huge impact on the efficiency and stability of the method. However, while the theory behind the multipole expansion has been broadly researched, the clustering process itself and its effect on the FMM has been neglected in comparison. Most of the time, for example, it is implicitly assumed that the elements of the mesh have about the same size, which is often not the case in practical applications, e.g., when calculating the sound field around the human head. In this study we compare different types of clustering approaches with respect to stability and efficiency of the underlying FMM applied to meshes that have uniform as well as non-uniform element sizes. Also, some examples are provided for cases where a wrong clustering can lead to numerical problems and instabilities of the FMM-BEM.

</details>


### [121] [PDE Identification Using Noise Adaptive Differentiation in Strong Form (S-IDENT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31776)
*Roy Y. He,Sung Ha Kang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出S-IDENT方法，通过Savitzky--Golay微分结合SURE自适应窗口处理噪声数据，在强形式框架下识别偏微分方程，兼具弱形式方法的鲁棒性和强形式字典的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDE识别方法分为强形式和弱形式两类：强形式对噪声敏感但字典更通用；弱形式鲁棒但受限于积分变换。本文旨在弥合两者差距，在保持强形式字典表达能力的同时提升对噪声的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 使用Savitzky--Golay（SG）微分结合Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）自适应选择窗口长度进行降噪微分；2) 采用强形式字典（含线性和非线性项）进行PDE识别；3) 通过修剪（trimming）和残差缩减模型选择进一步稳定和优化识别过程。

Result: S-IDENT能在比现有强形式方法更高的噪声水平下成功识别非线性PDE，结果与弱形式方法相当。尽管强形式字典更大且条件数更差，S-IDENT并未显著受字典规模组合增长的影响。

Conclusion: S-IDENT有效结合了强形式字典的通用性和弱形式方法的噪声鲁棒性，为从噪声时空轨迹中识别PDE提供了一种有竞争力的新方案。

Abstract: We explore identifying partial differential equations (PDEs) from noisy observations of single time-space trajectories. Recent developments show the benefits of identifying PDEs in their weak forms. We investigate the use of differential Strong-form dictionaries for PDE IDENTification (S-IDENT), which enables finding more general linear and nonlinear PDEs. Building on an extensive exploration of integral-type denoised differentiation approaches, we propose to use Savitzky--Golay (SG) differentiation with an adaptive window length chosen based on Stein's Unbiased Risk Estimate (SURE). This offers a guaranteed order of accuracy while producing estimators with minimal variance. The identification process is further refined and stabilized through trimming and reduction-in-residual model selection. Numerical evidence shows that S-IDENT can successfully identify nonlinear PDEs at higher levels of noise than existing strong-form methods, while also yielding results comparable to weak-form approaches. We further verify the effectiveness of S-IDENT through comparisons with various strategies to approximate differential features. We provide numerical evidence that general differential-form dictionaries are larger and more ill-conditioned than those used for weak-form identification, yet S-IDENT does not significantly suffer from this combinatorial increase in dictionary size.

</details>


### [122] [Complementary families of approximating polynomials with applications to finite element methods applied to differential equations of arbitrary even spatial order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31829)
*Peter K Moore*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两类互补多项式族来生成任意 $m \ge 0$ 且阶数 $p \ge 2m+2$ 的 $C^m$ 有限元基函数：一类是确保跨单元 $C^m$ 连续的 Hermite 样条节点基函数，另一类是由 Legendre 多项式导数与二项式幂乘积导出的内部（气泡）基函数；建立了二者关系，给出了插值误差公式和超收敛结果，并通过数值实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任意 $m \ge 0$ 和阶数 $p \ge 2m+2$ 的系统化构造 $C^m$ 连续有限元基函数提供统一框架，并建立插值误差分析和超收敛理论。

Method: 在 $[0,1]$ 上给出 Hermite 样条显式公式；在 $[-1,1]$ 上利用 Legendre 多项式 $m+1$ 阶导数乘以二项式幂构造气泡函数（与超球多项式相关）；利用超球多项式根和区间端点构造插值算子；通过 Lagrange 插值扩展证明误差公式；在 $H^{m+1}$ 范数下证明线性情形超收敛及插值与有限元解的渐近等价性。

Result: 给出了两类多项式族的显式表达式及其关系；推导了推广到任意 $m$ 的插值误差公式；证明了线性情形下 $H^{m+1}$ 中的超收敛结果及插值与有限元解的渐近等价性；数值实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阶 $C^m$ 有限元基函数构造提供了系统方法，统一并推广了 $m=0$ 和 $m=1$ 的已有结果，建立了完备的误差分析和超收敛理论。

Abstract: Complementary families of polynomials are introduced to generate $C^m$ finite element basis functions of order $p \geq 2m+2$ for arbitrary $m \ge 0$. One family consists of the Hermite splines that serve as the nodal basis functions by ensuring $C^m$ continuity across element boundaries. Explicit formulas for these splines for any $m \ge 0$ are presented on the canonical interval $[0,1]$. The second family is derived on the interval $[-1,1]$ from derivatives of order $m+1$ of the Legendre polynomials of degree $p-m-1$ multiplied by binomial powers of degree $m+1$ at -1 and 1, respectively. These polynomials, related to the ultraspherical polynomials, serve as the interior or bubble basis functions. A relationship between the two families of polynomials is demonstrated. For a particular $m$ and $p$, an interpolant is constructed using these basis pairs together with the roots of the related ultraspherical polynomial and the interval endpoints. A formula for the interpolation error that extends the results for $m=0$ and $m=1$ is given. To prove the formula extensions of the Lagrange interpolants are introduced. A superconvergence result along with the related asymptotic equivalence of the interpolant and finite element solution is proved in the linear case in $H^{m+1}$. Computational results demonstrate the theory for a model problem.

</details>


### [123] [Numerical Study of Eigenvector Deflation to Accelerate the WaveHoltz Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31842)
*Daniel Appelo,William D. Henshaw,Zhichao Peng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征向量消去法加速WaveHoltz求解Helmholtz方程的方法，发现消去靠近驱动频率的特征向量可大幅减少迭代次数，且使用SVD压缩可降低存储成本，在二维算例中仅需2个右端项即可达到盈亏平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WaveHoltz方法在高频Helmholtz方程求解中固定点迭代收敛缓慢（$d$维空间需$\mathcal{O}(ω^{2d})$次迭代），亟需加速策略以提升计算效率。

Method: 采用两种特征向量消去方式：直接特征向量消去(DEVD)将强迫项和迭代向量在消去集上投影，以及增广Krylov特征向量消去(AUKED)结合消去共轭梯度(DCG)、增广GMRES(AGMRES)和增广(回收)BICGSTAB(ABICGSTAB)。利用EigenWave高效计算特征对，并对结构化网格上的特征向量矩阵进行SVD压缩以减少存储。

Result: 在二维问题中，当消去向量数量随$ω$二次增长时，渐近收敛率基本保持不变；在单曲线网格（求和分部算子离散）和重叠网格上的数值实验验证了方法的稳健性和高效性，消去求解器仅需2个右端项即可弥补预计算特征向量的成本。

Conclusion: 特征向量消去能有效加速WaveHoltz方法求解高频Helmholtz方程，且通过SVD压缩和适当的消去策略可实现实际计算中的盈亏平衡。

Abstract: We present a numerical study of eigenvector deflation as a means of accelerating the WaveHoltz method for solving the Helmholtz equation. For energy-conserving (Dirichlet or Neumann) boundary conditions the WaveHoltz fixed-point iteration converges slowly at high frequency, requiring approximately $\mathcal{O}(ω^{2d})$ iterations in $d$ dimensions. We show that deflating the eigenvectors whose eigenvalues lie nearest the driving frequency substantially reduces iteration counts, and we examine two ways of incorporating the eigenvectors: direct eigenvector deflation (DEVD), in which the forcing and iterate are projected against the deflation set, and augmented-Krylov eigenvector deflation (AUKED) using deflated conjugate gradient (DCG), augmented GMRES (AGMRES), and augmented (recycled) BICGSTAB (ABICGSTAB). The required eigenpairs can be computed efficiently with the EigenWave approach, and we demonstrate, in two dimensions, that when the number of deflation vectors grows quadratically with $ω$ the asymptotic convergence rate remains essentially constant. Because the eigenvectors on structured grids are naturally represented as matrices, we further apply SVD-based compression to reduce their storage. Numerical experiments on single curvilinear grids discretized with summation-by-parts operators, and on overset grids illustrate the robustness and efficiency of the approach, with the deflated solver breaking even against the undeflated solver after as few as two right-hand sides, when accounting for the cost of precomputing the eigenvectors.

</details>


### [124] [Isogeometric discretizations for the spectrum of the Laplace operator: outlier-free spline bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31843)
*Damiano Ricci,Carla Manni,Hendrik Speleers*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了等几何分析中用于消除拉普拉斯算子谱逼近异常值的最优样条子空间，给出了同时对角化质量和刚度矩阵的基函数构造方法，并证明了该基是无异常值的，同时提供了数值构造过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在等几何Galerkin离散化中，拉普拉斯算子谱逼近存在异常值问题，需要一种高效且具有良好计算和谱性质的工具来消除这些异常值。

Method: 通过刻画具有B样条支撑结构且质量和刚度矩阵可同时对角化的基函数，推导特征值的闭式表达式，并设计数值构造过程。

Result: 所构造的基的质量矩阵和刚度矩阵具有显式已知的特征值闭式表达式，从而保证基是无异常值的。

Conclusion: 该基构造方法兼具B样条支撑结构、同时对角化能力和无异常值特性，为等几何分析谱逼近提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Optimal spline subspaces are an elegant and efficient tool to remove spurious outliers in isogeometric Galerkin discretizations for the approximation of the spectrum of the Laplace operator. For practical purposes, it is valuable to have a basis construction for such spaces with good computational and spectral properties. We provide a characterization of the bases that enjoy a B-spline-like support structure and whose mass and stiffness matrices are simultaneously diagonalizable. It turns out that these mass and stiffness matrices admit explicitly known closed-form expressions for their eigenvalues, implying that the considered bases are outlier-free. A numerical procedure for constructing such bases is also presented.

</details>


### [125] [Hidden Accuracy and Superconvergence Analysis of Central Discontinuous Galerkin Methods on Overlapping Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31931)
*Manting Peng,Kailiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了重叠网格上中心间断伽辽金（CDG）方法求解线性双曲方程的首次严格超收敛理论，通过提出投影-校正框架，揭示了隐藏的超收敛机制（一维渐近弱残差相消与多维高阶聚合相消），严格证明了$\mathbb{Q}^k$ CDG格式在超收敛点处$\mathcal{O}(h^{k+2})$的逐点超收敛性，并发现了$\mathcal{O}(h^{\min\{2k+1,k+3\}})$的单元平均值超收敛估计，同时将理论推广至全离散显式Runge-Kutta CDG格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Liu、Shu和Zhang已建立了均匀笛卡尔网格上$\mathbb{Q}^k$ CDG格式的最优$L^2$收敛性，但其观测到的$\mathcal{O}(h^{k+2})$逐点超收敛现象一直缺乏严格证明，根本障碍在于CDG重叠网格结构丧失了标准单网格伽辽金正交性。

Method: 提出投影-校正框架：一维情形利用渐近弱残差相消机制，多维情形引入高阶聚合相消（HOCA）机制，克服耦合主-对偶方向残差带来的分析挑战，恢复标准变分公式中缺失的关键误差相消性质。

Result: 严格证明了$\mathbb{Q}^k$ CDG格式在超收敛点处离散$\ell^\infty$范数下$\mathcal{O}(h^{k+2})$的逐点超收敛性；发现了校正初始化下$\mathcal{O}(h^{\min\{2k+1,k+3\}})$的单元平均值超收敛估计；将理论推广至全离散显式Runge-Kutta CDG格式，建立了基于重构的后处理估计。数值实验验证了理论阶数的锐利性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的超收敛理论填补了CDG方法超收敛分析的空白，为重叠网格上间断伽辽金方法的误差分析提供了新框架。

Abstract: This paper establishes the first rigorous superconvergence theory for semidiscrete and fully discrete central discontinuous Galerkin (CDG) methods for linear hyperbolic equations on overlapping meshes. While the optimal $L^2$ convergence of $\mathbb{Q}^k$ CDG schemes was established on uniform Cartesian meshes by Liu, Shu, and Zhang [ SIAM J. Numer. Anal.}, 56 (2018), pp. 520--541], their observed $\mathcal{O}(h^{k+2})$ pointwise superconvergence has remained unproven, due to the loss of standard single-mesh Galerkin orthogonality inherent in the CDG overlapping structure.
  To overcome this fundamental barrier, we introduce a projection-correction framework that identifies a hidden superconvergent mechanism: an asymptotic weak residual cancellation in one dimension, and a high-order cancellation-by-aggregation (HOCA) mechanism in multiple dimensions. This HOCA approach overcomes the analytical challenge posed by coupled primal-dual directional residuals, recovering critical error cancellation properties absent from the standard variational formulation. Consequently, we provide the rigorous proof of the conjectured $\mathcal{O}(h^{k+2})$ pointwise superconvergence in the discrete $\ell^{\infty}$ norm across all superconvergent points. Furthermore, we reveal that under a systematically corrected initialization, this framework yields a previously undiscovered, stronger cell-average superconvergence estimate of order $\mathcal{O}(h^{\min\{2k+1,k+3\}})$. The theory is extended to fully discrete explicit Runge--Kutta CDG schemes, where stagewise corrected errors are constructed to preserve spatial superconvergence up to temporal truncation errors, yielding a stable reconstruction-based postprocessing estimate. Numerical experiments in one and two spatial dimensions confirm the sharpness of the theoretical rates.

</details>


### [126] [GQL-Based Physical-Constraint-Preserving High-Order Finite Difference Schemes for Special Relativistic Hydrodynamics in Arbitrary Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31992)
*Linfeng Xu,Shengrong Ding,Kailiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对高精度模拟特殊相对论流体动力学（RHD）中因违反物理约束（正密度/压力、亚光速）导致的数值崩溃问题，提出了一种基于几何拟线性化（GQL）表示和Zalesak型通量修正传输（FCT）的物理约束保持（PCP）通量限制框架。通过有理立体投影参数化将最坏情况最小化转化为广义Rayleigh商问题，通过求解小规模对称特征值问题获得最优限制参数，并在多维情形引入松弛变体以降低计算成本。1D至3D数值实验验证了该方法在保持设计精度的同时，能鲁棒地强制执行物理可行性并清晰分辨间断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度模拟特殊相对论流体动力学时，在强间断附近容易违反正密度/压力和亚光速等物理约束，导致数值崩溃。现有方法在鲁棒性、效率和精度保持之间难以兼顾，亟需一种既能严格保持物理约束又不牺牲高阶格式精度和计算效率的通量限制框架。

Method: 1) 利用几何拟线性化（GQL）将非线性RHD约束等价重构为一系列线性不等式；2) 将Zalesak型FCT更新集成到直接作用于守恒变量的标量风格限制器中；3) 通过有理立体投影参数化GQL法向量，将最坏情况最小化转化为广义Rayleigh商问题，通过求解小规模对称特征值问题（1D:2×2; d维:(d+1)×(d+1)）显式非迭代地确定限制参数；4) 在多维情形引入松弛变体以降低计算成本。

Result: 1D至3D的广泛数值测试（包括超相对论Riemann问题和天体物理喷流）表明：所提方法能鲁棒地强制执行物理可容许性，清晰分辨间断，并在光滑解区域保持设计阶精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于GQL-FCT的PCP通量限制框架为高精度RHD模拟提供了一种鲁棒且高效的物理约束保持方案，其核心创新在于将非线性约束的满足问题转化为可显式求解的特征值问题，避免了迭代过程，兼具理论严格性和计算实用性。

Abstract: High-order accurate simulations of special relativistic hydrodynamics (RHD) are prone to numerical breakdown if intrinsic physical constraints (positive rest-mass density/pressure and subluminal velocity) are violated near strong discontinuities. In this work, we develop a robust and efficient physical-constraint-preserving (PCP) flux-limiting framework for high-order schemes, using finite-difference WENO as a representative example. By leveraging the geometric quasilinearization (GQL) representation, which equivalently reformulates the nonlinear RHD constraints into a family of linear inequalities, we integrate a Zalesak-type Flux-Corrected Transport (FCT) update into a scalar-style limiter that acts directly on conservative variables. A critical innovation is the explicit, non-iterative determination of limiting parameters via a rational stereographic parameterization of the GQL normal vector. This technique transforms the required worst-case minimization over auxiliary variables into a generalized Rayleigh-quotient formulation, allowing the optimal parameters to be obtained by solving small symmetric eigenvalue problems ($2\times2$ in 1D; $(d+1)\times(d+1)$ in $d$ dimensions). Relaxed variants are further introduced to reduce computational costs in multidimensions while retaining the PCP guarantee. Extensive numerical benchmarks ranging from 1D to 3D, including ultra-relativistic Riemann problems and astrophysical jets, demonstrate that the proposed method robustly enforces physical admissibility, sharply resolves discontinuities, and maintains design-order accuracy for smooth solutions.

</details>

Abstract: This is an update of an expository article on the geometrization of orbital integrals of spherical Hecke functions on reductive groups over non-archimedean local fields, appeared in Proceedings of ICCM 2019. Compared to the published version, we add a last section on an example in SL3 case.

### [128] [Image of Regular Unipotent under a Representation of $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{C})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31398)
*Dibyendu Biswas*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了$\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{C})$的任意有限维不可约多项式表示下正则幂零元的像，将其等价为$\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{C})$-模的特定不可约表示复合分解，并给出了该分解的显式Jordan分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{C})$的不可约多项式表示中正则幂零元的像，这等价于将某些不可约表示的复合作为$\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{C})$-模进行分解。

Method: 将问题转化为$\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{C})$-模的分解问题，通过分析不可约表示的复合结构，给出显式分解。

Result: 给出了该表示的显式分解及其Jordan分解。

Conclusion: 对$\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{C})$的不可约多项式表示中正则幂零元的像进行了完整的描述和分解。

Abstract: We study the image of a regular unipotent element under any finite-dimensional irreducible polynomial representations of $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{C})$. This problem is equivalent to decomposing certain compositions of irreducible representations as $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{C})$-modules. We give an explicit decomposition of this finding, its Jordan decomposition.

</details>


### [129] [On the trilinear and Ginzburg-Rallis models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31402)
*Xinrui Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了非阿基米德局部域（特征零）上GL₆(k)的诱导表示中Ginzburg-Rallis模型与三线性模型之间的同构条件，并给出了这两类模型非零的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解GL₆(k)的抛物诱导表示中Ginzburg-Rallis模型与三线性模型之间的关系，并建立非零性判据。

Method: 本文针对特征零的非阿基米德局部域k，考虑GL₆(k)从[2³]型抛物子群诱导的表示，给出了Ginzburg-Rallis模型与诱导数据的三线性模型同构的充分条件，并推导了非零性判据。

Result: 给出了GL₆(k)的[2³]型抛物诱导表示中Ginzburg-Rallis模型与三线性模型同构的充分条件，以及这两类模型的非零判据。

Conclusion: 该结果为Ginzburg-Rallis模型与三线性模型之间的联系提供了理论依据，并为相关表示论问题中的非零性判定提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a non-archimedean local field of characteristic zero. We give sufficient conditions under which the Ginzburg-Rallis models of the induced representations of $\mathrm{GL}_6(k)$ from a parabolic subgroup of type $[2^3]$ are isomorphic to the trilinear models of the inducing data. We also give nonvanishing criterion for these trilinear models and Ginzburg-Rallis models.

</details>


### [130] [A generalization of partition identities of Göllnitz-Gordon, Rogers-Ramanujan and Nandi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31821)
*Motoki Takigiku,Shunsuke Tsuchioka*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出了$A^{(2)}_{\textrm{odd}}$类型第二级标准模的Andrews-Gordon级数，并证明了除$A^{(2)}_{6n+3}$情况外的相应和积恒等式，这些恒等式推广了Göllnitz-Gordon（模8）、Rogers-Ramanujan（模5）和（部分）Nandi（模14）恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典的Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式及其推广（如Göllnitz-Gordon和Nandi恒等式）到更一般的李代数$A^{(2)}_{\textrm{odd}}$框架下的标准模表示中。

Method: 提出$A^{(2)}_{\textrm{odd}}$类型第二级标准模的Andrews-Gordon级数，并通过分析和组合方法推导相应的和积恒等式。

Result: 成功证明了除$A^{(2)}_{6n+3}$外所有情况下的和积恒等式，这些恒等式统一推广了模5、模8和模14的经典恒等式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了李代数表示论与经典q-级数恒等式之间的新联系，但$A^{(2)}_{6n+3}$情况仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We propose Andrews-Gordon type series for certain level 2 standard modules of type $A^{(2)}_{\textrm{odd}}$, and prove the corresponding sum-product identities except for $A^{(2)}_{6n+3}$. These identities generalize the identities of Göllnitz-Gordon (mod 8), Rogers-Ramanujan (mod 5) and (partially) Nandi (mod 14).

</details>

### [131] [Search for physics beyond the standard model in four and three top quark production events using proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30862)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb^{-1}）中寻找超出标准模型的物理信号，通过分析四顶夸克和三顶夸克产生事件，在有效场论框架下约束了六种Wilson系数，排除了质量在400 GeV至1.6 TeV范围内的窄共振态，并提取了顶夸克Yukawa耦合（含CP偶和CP奇贡献）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理，特别是通过四顶夸克和三顶夸克产生过程来研究可能的新相互作用。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb^{-1}），选取含有两个同号轻子、三个或四个轻子（电子和/或μ子）的事件，在标准模型有效场理论（SMEFT）框架下约束六种Wilson系数，同时搜索窄共振态并提取顶夸克Yukawa耦合。

Result: 在SMEFT框架下约束了六种涉及第三代夸克间相互作用或顶夸克与Higgs玻色子相互作用的Wilson系数；排除了质量在400 GeV至1.6 TeV范围内的窄共振态（依赖其自旋和色态）；提取了包含CP偶和CP奇贡献的顶夸克Yukawa耦合。

Conclusion: 该分析对超出标准模型的新物理给出了严格的限制，覆盖了有效场论算符、新共振粒子和顶夸克Yukawa耦合等多个方面。

Abstract: A search for physics beyond the standard model using four and three top quark production events is reported. The analyzed proton-proton collision data were recorded at 13 TeV with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC in 2016$-$2018 and correspond to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Events with two same-sign, three, or four leptons (electrons and/or muons) are selected. Constraints on six Wilson coefficients that modify interactions between four third-generation quarks or between top quarks and the Higgs boson in the standard model effective field theory framework are derived. The data are further used to exclude narrow topphilic heavy resonances in the mass ranges between 400 GeV and 1.6 TeV depending on their spin and color states. Finally, the top quark Yukawa coupling is extracted, considering both $CP$-even and $CP$-odd contributions.

</details>


### [132] [Measurement of $J/ψ$-jet correlations in $pp$ and Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\text{NN}}}=5.02$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31375)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS探测器在LHC采集的5.02 TeV Pb+Pb和pp碰撞数据，通过测量J/ψ介子与喷注活动的关联（区分孤立/非孤立产生以及直接/非直接产生），系统研究了QGP对粲偶素的压制效应。结果表明J/ψ在60 GeV高横动量下仍存在显著压制，且孤立与非孤立J/ψ的压制存在差异，为理解粲偶素产生机制及其介质中抑制提供了新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲偶素（如J/ψ）在重离子碰撞中与夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）相互作用而强烈抑制，但抑制机制尚需深入理解。为约束产生机制，需量化J/ψ产生与喷注活动的关联。

Method: 在双缪子衰变道测量J/ψ产生，通过anti-kT算法(R=0.2, pT>20 GeV)将J/ψ匹配到去除J/ψ信号的喷注，区分离散/非孤立以及直接/非直接（通过b夸克衰变）产生。使用ATLAS探测器记录的Pb+Pb(1.82 nb⁻¹)和pp(0.26 fb⁻¹)数据，测量截面、产额和核修正因子R_{AA}。

Result: J/ψ在pT高达60 GeV仍存在强压制；孤立与非孤立J/ψ的抑制程度存在差异；报告了孤立分数和非直接产生分数在pp和Pb+Pb碰撞中的结果。

Conclusion: 这些结果对粲偶素产生机制及其在QGP中的介质抑制提供了新约束。

Abstract: Yields of charmonia, bound states of $c\bar{c}$ quarks, are observed to be strongly suppressed in heavy-ion collisions relative to proton-proton collisions as a result of their interaction with the quark-gluon plasma produced in such collisions. Understanding the mechanisms responsible for this suppression requires a detailed understanding of charmonium production. To constrain the production mechanisms, it is essential to quantify how charmonium production correlates with jet activity. Measurements of $J/ψ$ production in the dimuon decay channel are presented for inclusive $J/ψ$ mesons and for $J/ψ$ mesons that are either isolated or not isolated from jets, separately for prompt and non-prompt production. Non-isolated $J/ψ$ mesons are defined by matching $J/ψ$ candidates to anti-$k_t$ jets with $R=0.2$ and $p_\mathrm{T}>20$ GeV, reconstructed without signal from the $J/ψ$ meson, allowing the quantification of jet activity accompanying $J/ψ$ production. The analysis uses Pb+Pb and $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\text{NN}}}=5.02$ TeV recorded by the ATLAS experiment at the LHC, corresponding to integrated luminosities of 1.82 nb$^{-1}$ and 0.26 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. The $J/ψ$ yields in Pb+Pb, cross sections in $pp$, and nuclear modification factors are presented for non-isolated, isolated, and inclusive $J/ψ$ up to $p_\mathrm{T}=60$ GeV. Isolated fractions and non-prompt fractions are reported in both $pp$ and Pb+Pb collisions. Strong $J/ψ$ suppression is observed to persist up to the highest measured $p_\mathrm{T}$ values, with differences observed between non-isolated, isolated, and inclusive $J/ψ$. These results provide new constraints on charmonium production mechanisms and their in-medium suppression.

</details>
