### [133] [Magnetic Bloch Oscillations in Odd-Wave Magnets and the Nonlinear Edelstein Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30732)
*Jonas Habel,Johannes Knolle*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出“磁布洛赫振荡”概念，指出在奇宇称波磁体中，磁化强度可在外电场作用下产生振荡（类似电荷布洛赫振荡），最终弛豫至稳态Edelstein值。通过一维p波磁体模型和二维推广，解析和数值方法证明了该效应，并发现Edelstein磁化强度在电场中呈现非线性依赖。文章进一步提出可通过太赫兹亚周期光波谱学中的高次谐波产生来实验检测该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统布洛赫振荡研究聚焦于电荷输运，但载流子同时携带自旋，因此自然追问：磁性观测量中是否也能存在布洛赫振荡？本文旨在探索奇宇称波磁体中的磁化强度振荡行为。

Method: 采用解析和数值方法研究最小一维p波磁体模型，并推广至二维系统；通过分析磁化强度在外电场下的动力学行为，揭示磁布洛赫振荡的存在；利用太赫兹光谱学中的高次谐波生成作为实验探测手段。

Result: 发现了奇宇称波磁体中磁化强度的布洛赫振荡现象，振荡最终弛豫至稳态Edelstein值；Edelstein磁化强度在电场中呈现非线性行为；提出可通过太赫兹高次谐波产生实验观测该效应。

Conclusion: 磁布洛赫振荡是自旋-电荷耦合在非常规磁体中的一种非平衡特征信号，为理解自旋输运和磁性量子振荡提供了新视角，并给出了可行的实验检测方案。

Abstract: Bloch oscillations (BOs) are a quantum phenomenon in which electrons subjected to an electric field in a periodic potential exhibit an oscillating current without a net drift. In real conductors, scattering reduces the coherence required for BOs driving the system toward a steady state with a DC current. While previous studies have focused on charge transport, charge carriers also possess spin, raising the question of whether BOs can emerge in magnetic observables. Here, we show that the magnetization of odd-wave magnets can undergo BOs before relaxing to the steady-state Edelstein value, a phenomenon we term $\textit{magnetic}$ BOs. Using analytical and numerical methods, we demonstrate this effect in a minimal one-dimensional model of a p-wave magnet and generalize it to two dimensions. Our analysis further reveals that the Edelstein magnetization is generically nonlinear in the applied electric field. Finally, we argue that magnetic BOs can be detected in materials through higher-harmonic generation in THz sub-cycle lightwave spectroscopy. Magnetic BOs provide a genuine non-equilibrium signature of spin-charge coupling in unconventional magnets.

</details>


### [134] [The competition between low-temperature kinks and magnons at the vicinity of the deconfinement transition point in 1D easy-axis XXZ ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31000)
*P. N. Bibikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究一维易轴XXZ铁磁体退禁闭相变点附近的有序相和量子超临界低温区域，提出根据主导最低能激发的解释：两个有序相由磁振子主导，量子超临界区由扭结主导，并详细处理了伊辛模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一维易轴XXZ铁磁体在退禁闭相变点附近的有序相和量子超临界低温区域的性质，并基于最低能激发对它们进行物理解释。

Method: 通过分析不同区域（有序相和量子超临界区）的最低能激发类型（磁振子 vs 扭结），建立激发与相行为之间的对应关系，并以伊辛模型为例进行详细处理。

Result: 两个有序相由磁振子（magnons）主导，而量子超临界低温区域由扭结（kinks）主导。

Conclusion: 退禁闭相变点附近的低温物理行为可以通过不同最低能激发进行统一解释，伊辛模型作为特例验证了这一框架。

Abstract: Studying the ordered phases and quantum supercritical low-temperature regime at the vicinity of the deconfinement transition point in 1D easy-axis XXZ ferromagnet, we suggest their interpretations according to the corresponding dominant lowest-energy excitations. We show, that the two ordered phases are governed by magnons, while the quantum supercritical regime is governed by kinks. Within this framework the Ising model is treated in detail.

</details>


### [135] [Investigation of the $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice: A study with projected entangled-pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31021)
*Litao Ma,Wei-Lin Tu,Didier Poilblanc,Ji-Yao Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用无限投影纠缠对态(PEPS)方法系统研究了三角晶格上受挫J1-J2海森堡模型，发现了从120°奈尔态到量子自旋液体(QSL)的直接转变，并揭示了QSL相的无隙(临界)性质，最自然地支持U(1)狄拉克自旋液体图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格上受挫J1-J2海森堡模型中量子自旋液相的性质仍是一个悬而未决且活跃争论的问题，需要更精确的数值方法进行系统研究。

Method: 采用无限投影纠缠对态(PEPS)方法，在不同对称性约束下系统研究模型，包括构建全对称拓扑Z2共振价键态以及更大键维数(D≤7)的Z2对称PEPS，并进行波函数优化。

Result: 发现了从120°奈尔态到QSL在J2/J1≈0.08处的直接转变；证实PEPS框架能准确捕获条状反铁磁相；发现全对称拓扑Z2共振价键态(D=3)具有合理好的变分能量；但更大键维数的Z2对称PEPS在优化后出现自旋子凝聚和拓扑涡旋激发禁闭，排除了Z2拓扑序。

Conclusion: 三角晶格J1-J2模型中的QSL相很可能是无隙(临界)的，最自然地与U(1)狄拉克自旋液体图像一致，而非Z2拓扑序。

Abstract: The nature of the quantum spin liquid (QSL) phase in the frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice remains an open and actively debated problem. In this work, we employ the infinite projected entangled-pair state (PEPS) to systematically investigate the model under different symmetry constraints. Our simulations reveal a direct transition from the $120^\circ$ Néel state to a putative QSL at $J_2/J_1\approx 0.08$, signaled by the collapse of magnetic order. We further show that, through either an appropriate unitary rotation or spontaneous spin long-range order, the stripe antiferromagnetic phase can also be accurately captured within the infinite PEPS framework. A central focus of our study is the role played by the PEPS symmetry in approximating the QSL ground-state sandwiched between the two magnetic phases. We first found that a fully-symmetric topological $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Resonating Valence Bond state, which can be written as a simple PEPS with bond dimension $D=3$, exhibits a reasonably good variational energy. Motivated by this finding, we have further constructed generic $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric PEPS of larger bond dimension (up to $D=7$). We found that, under wavefunction optimization, spinons condense and, simultaneously, topological vison excitations get confined, hence precluding $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological order. This strongly indicates the gapless (or critical) nature of the QSL phase, which is most naturally consistent with a U(1) Dirac spin liquid scenario.

</details>


### [136] [Nonlinear topological laser based on multipole insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31067)
*Zi-Yuan Li,Zi-Xiang Hu,Qi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了二维BBH模型中的高阶拓扑绝缘体，通过将激光模式限制在拓扑角态或边态，展示了长时间演化后的稳定光激发主要由模型哈密顿量的拓扑性质决定。引入了角态衰减比τ₁、边角比τ₂和角间转移比χ等诊断参数来表征拓扑特征，并通过这些参数随跳跃参数γ/λ的突变揭示了拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为开发高阶拓扑激光器提供理论平台，通过研究BBH模型中的拓扑角态和边态的光学特性，探索利用拓扑态实现稳定激光激发的可能性。

Method: 以二维BBH模型为研究对象，通过受控增益将激光模式限制在选定的拓扑角态或边态；引入角态衰减比τ₁、边角比τ₂和角间转移比χ等诊断参数量化拓扑特征；通过调节晶格位点奇偶性调控角态局域位置。

Result: 长时间演化后的稳定光激发主要由哈密顿量的拓扑性质决定；τ₁和τ₂随跳跃参数γ/λ的突变直接揭示了拓扑相变；角态与边态之间的双稳态实现相对困难。

Conclusion: 高阶拓扑绝缘体中的拓扑角态和边态可用于实现稳定的激光激发，所提出的诊断参数为从系统动力学演化中提取拓扑特征提供了系统性工具包。

Abstract: Two-dimensional higher-order topological insulators (HOTIs), characterized by distinctive one-dimensional edge states and zero-dimensional corner states, provide an ideal platform for developing higher-order topological lasers. In this work, we systematically investigate the two-dimensional Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes (BBH) model, which hosts quantized quadrupole moments and topologically protected corner and edge states. By confining the lasing mode to selected topological corner or edge states under controlled gain, we demonstrate that the stable light excitation achieved after long-time evolution is predominantly determined by the topological properties of the model Hamiltonian. To characterize the system's topological features, we introduce several diagnostic ratios: the corner decay ratio $τ_{1}$ and edge-to-corner ratio $τ_{2}$ quantify the localization degree and spatial extent of corner states, respectively, while the inter-corner transfer ratio $χ$ measures the intensity transfer efficiency mediated by coherent edge-state dynamics. The abrupt changes in $τ_{1}$ and $τ_{2}$ as functions of the hopping parameter $γ/λ$ directly reveal topological phase transitions, providing a comprehensive toolkit for extracting topological signatures from the system's dynamical evolution. Additionally, modulating the lattice site parity enables flexible tuning of corner state localization positions, offering insights for device engineering. Our calculations reveal that achieving bistability between corner states and edge states is relatively challenging.

</details>


### [137] [Phonon-induced pseudogap phase in TiSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31298)
*Sotirios Fragkos,Nina Girotto Erhardt,Evgenia Symeonidou,Hibiki Orio,Dominique Descamps,Stéphane Petit,Polychronis Tsipas,Kai Rossnagel,Jakub Schusser,Athanasios Dimoulas,Samuel Beaulieu,Dino Novko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用时间分辨极紫外光电子能谱和第一性原理电子-声子计算，对三种典型1T相过渡金属硫属化物（TiSe₂、HfTe₂、ZrTe₂）在室温下的正常态电子结构进行了激发态能带映射，揭示了强电子-声子诱导热涨落对正常相电子结构的深刻影响，尤其是强耦合体系TiSe₂中存在声子诱导的赝隙相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解层状过渡金属硫属化物中电荷密度波等量子有序态，需要揭示其背后的正常态性质，但此前对强电子-声子耦合体系正常相的认识尚不充分。

Method: 使用时间分辨和角分辨极紫外光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）结合第一性原理电子-声子计算，对三种1T相TMDCs（TiSe₂、HfTe₂、ZrTe₂）在室温下进行激发态能带映射。

Result: 中等电子-声子耦合体系（HfTe₂、ZrTe₂）中热涨落仅导致小的谱展宽和能带重整化；强耦合体系TiSe₂中观察到软声子诱导的动量依赖谱权重抑制（赝隙），范围延伸至费米能级以上1 eV。

Conclusion: 建立了TiSe₂的正常相为声子诱导的赝隙相，由强CDW涨落主导，填补了TiSe₂相图中缺失的关键环节，并对其他强电子-声子耦合TMDCs具有普遍意义。

Abstract: To comprehend quantum ordered states, such as charge density waves (CDW), in layered transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs), it is essential to uncover their underlying normal states. Here, we use time- and angle-resolved extreme ultraviolet photoemission spectroscopy and ab initio electron-phonon calculations to perform excited state band mapping of three prototypical 1T TMDCs, i.e., TiSe$_2$, HfTe$_2$, and ZrTe$_2$, at room temperature. The results reveal the profound impact of strong electron-phonon-induced thermal fluctuations on the normal-phase electronic structure. Specifically, in the moderate electron-phonon coupling regime, as in HfTe$_2$ and ZrTe$_2$, thermal fluctuations only lead to small spectral broadening and band renormalization. In the strongly coupled case, exemplified by TiSe$_2$, we observe soft-phonon-induced, momentum-dependent suppression of spectral weight, i.e., pseudogaps - extending up to 1 eV above the Fermi level. Our work establishes the normal phase of TiSe$_2$ as a phonon-induced pseudogap phase governed by strong CDW fluctuations, thereby uncovering previously missing aspects of the TiSe$_2$ phase diagram, with broader implications for other TMDCs in the strong electron-phonon coupling regime.

</details>


### [138] [Ultrafast Fluence-Reversal Fingerprint of Fragile Kondo Hybridization in CePt$_2$In$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31361)
*Xin-Yi Tian,Qi-Yi Wu,Chen Zhang,Hao Liu,Yang Luo,Bo Chen,Ying Zhou,Zhong-Tuo Fu,Jin-Dong Bai,Chun-Hui Lyu,Zi-Jie Xu,Hai-Long Deng,Hai-Yun Liu,Jun He,Yu-Xia Duan,Jian-Qiao Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用飞秒泵浦-探测反射率技术研究了弱杂化Kondo晶格化合物CePt₂In₇中重准粒子的超快动力学，发现Kondo杂化间隙并非刚性电子结构，而是一种脆弱的、可被光致重新分布的许多体态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Kondo晶格中重准粒子形成的低能杂化间隙是刚性电子结构还是可动态重构的脆弱多体态，这对理解重费米子体系中的磁有序、量子临界性和非常规超导电性至关重要。

Method: 使用飞秒泵浦-探测反射率技术测量CePt₂In₇的超快动力学，结合Raman光谱独立识别相干光学声子作为内部晶格温度计，分析不同光通量下的电子弛豫行为。

Result: 在低温下观察到慢速准粒子弛豫通道，遵循Rothwarf-Taylor瓶颈响应，低能复合能标2Δ≈7.4 meV。电子振幅A_elec随温度从弱线性行为演变为Rothwarf-Taylor瓶颈抑制，最终在最低温出现反常的高通量增强，呈现通量反转指纹特征。

Conclusion: Kondo杂化间隙不是刚性的固定能隙，而是一种脆弱的、可在光激发下发生重新分布的多体电子态，该发现揭示了超快光学手段可用于探测Kondo杂化电子响应的光致重新分布。

Abstract: The emergence of heavy quasiparticles in a Kondo lattice is usually viewed as the formation of a low-energy hybridization gap. Whether this gap represents a rigid electronic structure or a fragile many-body state that can be dynamically reconfigured remains a central question for heavy-fermion systems near magnetic order, quantum criticality, and unconventional superconductivity. Here we use femtosecond pump-probe reflectivity to interrogate this problem in the weakly hybridized Kondo-lattice compound CePt$_2$In$_7$. At low fluence, a slow quasiparticle relaxation channel emerges below $T^* \sim$ 40 K and follows a Rothwarf-Taylor bottleneck response with a low-energy recombination scale 2$Δ\approx$ 7.4 meV. Coherent optical phonons, independently identified by Raman spectroscopy, act as an internal lattice thermometer and rule out large quasi-equilibrium lattice heating as the origin of the nonlinear electronic response. The phonon-free electronic amplitude $A_{\rm elec}$ reveals a fluence-reversal fingerprint: with cooling from the hybridization-crossover regime, the response evolves from weak-linear behavior to Rothwarf-Taylor-like bottleneck suppression and finally to anomalous high-fluence enhancement at the lowest temperatures. This reversal cannot be accounted for by a rigid fixed-gap bottleneck alone and instead identifies an ultrafast optical signature of photoinduced redistribution of a fragile Kondo-hybridized electronic response.

</details>


### [139] [Design Principles for Quasi-Isotropic Exchange in Rare-Earth Quantum Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31558)
*Kotaro Shimizu,Esteban Agustin Ghioldi,Filip Ronning,Cristian D. Batista*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过微扰理论研究了Ce³⁺和Yb³⁺基绝缘体中的超交换机制，揭示了各向同性交换主要来自基态Kramers二重态内的虚跃迁，而各向异性交换来自激发多重态过程，并提出了一种轨道设计原则：当基态二重态具有垂直于超交换面的最大角动量特征时，可促进准各向同性交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土量子材料中存在强自旋-轨道耦合导致各向异性交换相互作用，阻碍了准各向同性海森堡磁性的实现，需要阐明超交换的微观起源。

Method: 利用多轨道Hubbard模型的简并微扰理论，分析了Ce³⁺和Yb³⁺基绝缘体中共边八面体几何构型下的超交换机制。

Result: 各向同性交换主要来自基态Kramers二重态内的虚跃迁，各向异性交换来自激发多重态过程；基态二重态具有垂直于超交换面的最大角动量特征时促进准各向同性交换。

Conclusion: 建立了一个在稀土量子材料中工程化准各向同性相互作用的实用框架，与实验研究的Yb基绝缘体结果一致。

Abstract: Rare-earth quantum materials provide a promising platform for emergent phenomena ranging from quantum spin liquids with long-range entanglement to topological magnetic textures. However, the strong spin-orbit coupling that stabilizes their low-energy pseudospin degrees of freedom also tends to generate strongly anisotropic exchange interactions, complicating the realization of quasi-isotropic Heisenberg magnetism. Here we investigate the microscopic origin of superexchange in $\mathrm{Ce}^{3+}$- and $\mathrm{Yb}^{3+}$-based insulators with edge-sharing octahedral geometry. Using degenerate perturbation theory for a multiorbital Hubbard model, we show that isotropic exchange originates predominantly from virtual hopping within the ground-state Kramers doublet, whereas anisotropic interactions arise primarily from processes involving excited multiplets. This leads to a simple orbital design principle: quasi-isotropic exchange is promoted when the ground-state doublet has a strong maximal-angular-momentum character with respect to the quantization axis perpendicular to the superexchange plane spanned by rare-earth and ligand ions. We demonstrate this mechanism for both ideal and distorted geometries and show that it is broadly consistent with experimentally studied Yb-based insulators. Our results establish a practical framework for engineering quasi-isotropic interactions in rare-earth quantum materials.

</details>


### [140] [Local magnon modes studied by dynamic magnetic pair-density function analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31655)
*Shin-ichi Shamoto,Yukio Yasui,Kazuki Iida,Yasuhiro Inamura,Motoyuki Ishikado,Mitsutaka Nakamura,Lieh-Jeng Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次提出动态磁对密度函数(DymPDF)方法，通过对非极化非弹性中子散射测得的动态磁结构因子进行傅里叶变换，实现了实空间局域磁激发动力学分析，并展示了从声学到光学磁振子模式的能量依赖转变及自旋对关联的符号变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管动态磁结构因子S_M(Q,E)在磁激发研究中已有悠久历史，但一直缺乏其实空间对应量D_M(r,E)的研究，因此作者旨在填补这一空白，发展实空间局域磁动力学分析方法。

Method: 通过对非极化非弹性中子散射测得的动态磁结构因子S_M(Q,E)进行傅里叶变换得到D_M(r,E)；推导了简单磁模型在低能极限下的D_M(r,E)解析方程；利用FeTiO3和YBa2Cu3O6等代表性磁体进行模拟验证。

Result: 证明了D_M(r,E)的特征能量依赖性可揭示实空间局域磁振子模式，包括从声学到光学模式的转变；在纳米尺度实空间中观察到非周期条件下自旋对关联在特定能量处的符号变化。

Conclusion: 动态磁对密度函数(DymPDF)是一种独特且新颖的局域磁动力学研究方法，能够揭示传统动量空间方法无法获取的实空间局域磁激发信息。

Abstract: The dynamic magnetic pair-density function (DymPDF) $D_{\rm M}(r, E)$ is obtained via the Fourier transform of the dynamic magnetic structure factor, $S_{\rm M}(Q, E)$, which is measured using nonpolarized inelastic neutron scattering. While there is a long history of magnetic excitation studies with $S_{\rm M}(Q, E)$, there are no reports on $D_{\rm M}(r, E)$. In this study, we examine simple magnet models and representative magnet examples, such as FeTiO$_{3}$ and YBa$_{2}$Cu$_{3}$O$_{6}$, to investigate the real-space dynamics of $D_{\rm M}(r, E)$. We derive the $D_{\rm M}(r, E)$ equations for simple magnet models in a low energy limit. By comparing these equations to the simulations, we demonstrate the characteristic energy dependence of real-space local magnon modes, including the transition of the magnon mode from acoustic to optical. Our novel analysis reveals the local magnon modes accompanied by a sign change in each spin-pair correlation at a given energy in nanoscale real space even under non-periodic conditions. This method is unique for studying local magnetic dynamics.

</details>


### [141] [The exceptional origin of the strange metal and the LFL-HFL transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31810)
*Eoin Quinn*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个代数框架，将奇异金属态描述为Landau-Fermi液体与Hubbard-Fermi液体两种金属态竞争所产生的0+1维超共形浴，并导出了无参数的热力学关系式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为强关联电子奇异金属区建立代数框架，统一理解其非常规热力学和输运行为。

Method: 利用例外超共形代数D(2,1;α)的共形部分两种不同收缩：一种收缩到正则费米子（Landau-Fermi液体），另一种收缩到Hubbard算子代数（Hubbard-Fermi液体）。将奇异金属建模为这两种金属态竞争产生的0+1维超共形浴。

Result: 得到无参数预测：4π²γ⁻¹ = χₛ⁻¹ + χc⁻¹，联系了Sommerfeld系数与静态自旋和电荷磁化率。发现低温下LFL-HFL转变是不连续的，并画出了相应相图。

Conclusion: 该框架可连接微观晶格模型和相关绝缘体的现象学，为理解奇异金属提供代数基础。

Abstract: We propose an algebraic framework for the strange metal regime of strongly correlated electrons. We show that the exceptional superconformal algebra $D(2,1;α)$ admits two distinct contractions of its conformal sector: one to a pair of canonical fermions, the underlying degrees of freedom of the Landau-Fermi liquid (LFL), and one to the algebra of Hubbard operators, which characterise a distinct metallic regime, the Hubbard-Fermi liquid (HFL). We argue that competition between these two metallic states drives the emergence of the strange metal as a $0+1$D superconformal bath. We analyse the resulting thermodynamics, and obtain a parameter-free prediction, $4π^2γ^{-1} =χ_s^{-1} + χ_c^{-1}$, relating the Sommerfeld coefficient to the static spin and charge susceptibilities. We further show that the LFL-HFL transition is discontinuous at low temperature, owing to a degeneracy at the emergence of the HFL, and map out the resulting phase diagram. We connect the framework to microscopic lattice models and to the phenomenology of correlated insulators.

</details>

### [142] [Power-integral matrices over number fields: the Drazin inverse, pseudo-determinant, and numerical semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30876)
*Theo Chinn,Junshu Feng,Stephan Ramon Garcia,Hechun Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究数域上的矩阵，探讨其某个正幂次的所有元素落入对应整数环的条件，推广了之前的结果，并应用于数值半群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将关于矩阵幂次元素落在整数环中的问题从已有结果推广到更一般的数域和更广泛的矩阵类上，并寻找新的应用场景。

Method: 通过数域、整数环以及矩阵幂次的理论分析，结合数值半群的代数性质进行推广研究。

Result: 在多个方向上推广了已有结果，并获得了数值半群方面的应用。

Conclusion: 本文的推广工作不仅拓展了矩阵整数环性质的理论边界，还建立了与数值半群的联系，具有理论和应用双重价值。

Abstract: We investigate matrices with entries in a number field such that some positive power has all its entries in the corresponding ring of integers. Our work generalizes previous results in several directions and we find applications to numerical semigroups.

</details>


### [143] [On the exponential Diophantine equation $(a^n+1)(b^n+1)=x^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31223)
*Paulius Virbalas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了形如 $(a^n+1)(b^n+1)=x^2$ 的丢番图方程，证明了当 $a$ 和 $b$ 是同一整数 $t>1$ 的不同幂时，该方程在正整数范围内仅有唯一解；并在 $a$ 与 $b$ 互素且 $n$ 为偶数的条件下，给出了解的完整刻画。此外讨论了一些特殊情况并提出两个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程源自 Szalay (2000) 的工作，旨在探索特定形式的丢番图方程的解的结构与存在性，丰富指数丢番图方程理论。

Method: 采用数论分析方法，利用整除性、互素条件以及指数方程的标准技巧，针对不同参数情形（如 $a,b$ 为同一整数的不同幂、互素且 $n$ 为偶数等）进行分类讨论与证明。

Result: 证明了当 $a>1$ 且 $a,b$ 为 $t>1$ 的不同幂时，方程仅有唯一正整数解；在 $a,b$ 互素且 $n$ 为偶数时，给出了解的完整描述；还处理了若干其他特殊情况。

Conclusion: 该方程在特定条件下解的结构非常有限，文中结果推进了 Szalay 类型的丢番图方程研究，并提出了两个有待进一步验证的猜想。

Abstract: We study the Diophantine equation $(a^n+1)(b^n+1)=x^2$, which belongs to the family of equations originating from the work of Szalay in 2000. If $a>1$, it is shown that the equation of the title has only one solution in positive integers, when $a$ and $b$ are distinct powers of the same integer $t>1$. Also, a complete description of the solutions is obtained under the assumptions that $a$ and $b$ are coprime and $n$ is even. Several other special cases of the equation are considered, and two conjectures are proposed.

</details>


### [144] [On the Finiteness of Geometric Representations for Varieties over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31341)
*Yufan Luo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于有限域上带分歧界的代数基本群的半单表示有限性猜想，并在奇素数情形下对曲线（任意分歧界）和任意维数的驯化情形（D=0）给出了证明，同时在任意素数下证明了可提升到特征零的表示的有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Hiranouchi关于有限域上簇的Hermite-Minkowski型定理的启发，旨在建立带分歧界的算术基本群的半单表示的有限性结果，推广经典Hermite-Minkowski定理到正特征情形。

Method: 利用Hiranouchi定义的带分歧界的基本群π₁(X,D)，结合连续半单几何表示理论，分别处理曲线情形（任意分歧界D）和任意维数驯化情形（D=0），并通过提升到特征零的方法处理一般素数情形。

Result: 对奇素数p，证明了曲线（任意分歧界D）和任意维数驯化情形（D=0）下连续半单几何表示的有限性猜想；对任意素数p，证明了可提升到特征零的表示的有限性。

Conclusion: 建立了有限域上带分歧界的算术基本群的表示有限性定理，部分解决了该猜想，为理解正特征算术基本群的表示理论提供了新进展。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime number, and let $k$ be a finite field of characteristic different from $p$. Let $X$ be a normal geometrically connected variety over $k$, let $\overline X$ be a compactification of $X$, and let $Z=\overline X\setminus X$. Let $D$ be an effective Cartier divisor on $\overline X$ whose support is contained in $Z$. Motivated by Hiranouchi's Hermite--Minkowski type theorem for varieties over finite fields, we formulate a finiteness conjecture for continuous semisimple geometric representations
  $$
  π_1(X,D)\longrightarrow \operatorname{GL}_n(F),
  $$
  where $π_1(X,D)$ is Hiranouchi's fundamental group with ramification bounded by $D$, and $F$ is an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p$ endowed with the discrete topology. We prove this conjecture for odd $p$ in the following two cases: for curves with arbitrary ramification bound $D$, and for varieties of arbitrary dimension in the tame case, namely $D=0$. Furthermore, for arbitrary $p$, we prove the finiteness for those representations which admit a lift to characteristic zero.

</details>


### [145] [Sign Laws and Mock Theta Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31606)
*Manosij Ghosh Dastidar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Ramanujan三阶模拟θ函数ρ(q)的系数r(n)满足符号律：r(3m)>0, r(3m+1)≤0, r(3m+2)≤0，等号仅在n=2,4,8,11,20处成立。通过Watson恒等式将问题转化为比较模拟θ函数ω(q)与η商T(q)，利用有效单位根估计证明渐进符号律，并结合有限范围的整数算术验证完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Ramanujan三阶模拟θ函数ρ(q)的系数符号律，理解其系数分布的结构性规律。

Method: 利用Watson恒等式将ρ(q)与ω(q)和T(q)关联；通过有效单位根估计分析q=1、q=-1及三次本原单位根处的奇异性；结合渐进分析与有限范围整数算术验证。

Result: 证明了r(3m)>0, r(3m+1)≤0, r(3m+2)≤0的符号律，且等号仅出现在n=2,4,8,11,20五个值处。

Conclusion: 该单位根方法可推广到其他三阶模拟θ函数（如φ(q)和χ(q)）的符号律证明。

Abstract: Let \[ ρ(q)=\sum_{m\geq 0}\frac{q^{2m(m+1)}}{(1+q+q^2)(1+q^3+q^6)\cdots(1+q^{2m+1}+q^{4m+2})}
  =\sum_{n\geq 0}r(n)q^n \] be Ramanujan's third order mock theta function. We prove the sign law \[ r(3m)>0,\qquad r(3m+1)\leq 0,\qquad r(3m+2)\leq 0, \] with equality precisely at $n=2,4,8,11,20$. Watson's identity \[ 2ρ(q)+ω(q)=T(q) \] reduces the problem to comparing the mock theta function $ω(q)$ with the eta quotient \[ T(q)=3\frac{(q^6;q^6)_\infty^4}{(q^3;q^3)_\infty^2(q^2;q^2)_\infty}. \] We prove effective root-of-unity estimates for this difference. The polar contributions at $q=1$ cancel, the contribution at $q=-1$ is polynomially bounded, and the first surviving exponential term occurs at the primitive cubic roots of unity. It has the sign pattern \[ κ_0=\frac13\cos\fracπ{18}>0,\qquad κ_1=-\frac13\sin\frac{2π}{9}<0, \qquad κ_2=-\frac13\sin\frac\pi9<0. \] The resulting effective asymptotic proves the desired sign law for all sufficiently large $n$, and an exact integer-arithmetic verification completes the finite range. We conclude by indicating how the same root-of-unity method should lead to analogous sign laws for other third order mock theta functions, including $φ(q)$ and $χ(q)$.

</details>


### [146] [Diophantine rank stability and non-vanishing of $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31646)
*Marius Fischer,Asbjørn Christian Nordentoft*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于解析秩为0的模阿贝尔簇A，在|G|的所有素因子足够大时，存在无穷多个G-扩张F/Q使得A(F)有限；对于无例外素数的有理椭圆曲线或其乘积，该结论对任何有限交换群G成立。证明依赖于偶权全纯新形式的扭曲中心L值同时非零新结果，这些结果通过水平p进L函数的新构造获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究解析秩为0的模阿贝尔簇在数域扩张中秩不变（即A(F)仍然有限）的条件，特别是对非平凡有限交换群G的G-扩张。此前对这类问题的理解有限，本文试图给出更一般的条件。

Method: 1. 建立偶权全纯新形式的扭曲中心L值同时非零性结果；2. 利用水平p进L函数的新构造技术证明这些非零性；3. 将这些解析结果与模阿贝尔簇的算术性质（如Mazur的主猜想、Kolyvagin方法等）结合，推导出阿贝尔簇在G-扩张中秩保持为零的结论。

Result: 1. 对一般模阿贝尔簇A（解析秩0），当|G|的所有素因子足够大（依赖于A）时，存在无穷多个G-扩张使得A(F)有限。2. 对无例外素数的有理椭圆曲线（解析秩0）或其乘积，无需对|G|做任何假设，结论成立。

Conclusion: 解析秩为0的模阿贝尔簇在大多数有限交换扩张中保持秩为零，条件仅依赖于|G|的素因子大小或曲线的特殊性质。所得扭曲L值非零结果本身具有独立价值，可应用于其他算术问题。

Abstract: Let $A/\mathbb{Q}$ be a modular abelian variety of analytic rank $0$. If $G$ is a non-trivial finite abelian group such that all prime factors of $\lvert G \rvert$ are sufficiently large in terms of $A$, we show that there are infinitely many $G$-extensions $F/\mathbb{Q}$ such that $A(F)$ is finite. When $A$ is a rational elliptic curve of analytic rank zero with no exceptional primes, or the product of two such curves, the same conclusion holds without any assumptions on $|G|$. Our proof relies on new simultaneous non-vanishing results for twisted central $L$-values of even-weight holomorphic newforms. These results are obtained via novel constructions related to horizontal $p$-adic $L$-functions and are of independent interest.

</details>


### [147] [Tamagawa ratios and unbounded Selmer moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31649)
*Peter Koymans,Alexander Smith*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个预测Selmer群族平均大小是否有界的框架，并应用于$\mathbb{Q}$上某些几何阿贝尔簇族，给出了给定素数$\ell$下$\ell$-Selmer群平均大小无界族的猜想性刻画。当$\ell$-挠Galois模在族中恒定时，该刻画被证明是正确的。关键技术是Greenberg–Wiles公式，它将Selmer群与其对偶Selmer群的大小之比表示为局部因子乘积，由此给出Selmer群大小的纯局部下界，并猜想该下界在大多数情况下是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立预测Selmer群族平均大小是否有界的系统性框架，并给出几何阿贝尔簇族中$\ell$-Selmer群平均大小无界性的刻画。

Method: 利用Greenberg–Wiles公式将Selmer群与其对偶群的大小之比转化为局部因子的乘积，从而得到Selmer群大小的纯局部下界，并基于此建立预测框架。

Result: 给出了特定几何阿贝尔簇族的$\ell$-Selmer群平均大小无界性的猜想性刻画，并在$\ell$-挠Galois模恒定的情形下证明了该刻画成立。

Conclusion: Greenberg–Wiles公式提供的局部下界在大多数情况下是紧的，该框架为研究Selmer群的平均行为提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We develop a framework to predict whether a family of Selmer groups has average size that is bounded or unbounded. Applying this framework to certain geometric families of abelian varieties over $\mathbb{Q}$, we give a conjectural characterization of which such families have $\ell$-Selmer groups of unbounded average size for a given prime $\ell$. In the case that the $\ell$-torsion Galois module is constant across the family, we show that our characterization is correct.
  The key tool of our technique is the Greenberg--Wiles' formula, which expresses the ratio of the sizes of a Selmer group and the corresponding dual Selmer group as a product of local factors. This formula gives a purely local lower bound for the size of a Selmer group that we conjecture is close to sharp most of the time.

</details>


### [148] [Supersingular elliptic curves and twisting endomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31687)
*Sarah Arpin,Josep M. Miret,Jordi Pujolàs,Javier Valera*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将扭转向量自同态的概念推广到O-定向超奇异椭圆曲线，并提出寻找具有素数度ℓ扭转向量自同态的超奇异椭圆曲线的算法，用于计算其完全自同态环的基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Castryck-Panny-Vercauteran定义的扭转向量自同态概念到更一般的O-定向超奇异椭圆曲线场景，以扩展其应用范围。

Method: 提出一个算法，在有限域F_p上寻找具有素数度ℓ扭转向量自同态的超奇异椭圆曲线，并利用该结果计算其完全自同态环的一组基。

Result: 成功找到具有素数度ℓ扭转向量自同态的超奇异椭圆曲线，并计算了其完全自同态环的基。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了扭转向量自同态的概念，并为计算特定超奇异椭圆曲线的完全自同态环提供了有效算法。

Abstract: We generalize the notion of twisting endomorphisms, first defined by Castryck-Panny-Vercauteran, to the setting of $\mathcal{O}$-oriented supersingular elliptic curves. We give an algorithm to find supersingular elliptic curves over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with a twisting endomorphism of prime degree $\ell$, and we use it to compute a basis of their full endomorphism rings.

</details>


### [149] [Local-global compatibility at $p\neq\ell$ for torsion automorphic forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31698)
*Bence Hevesi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文为Scholze构造的自守群行列式证明了在$p \neq \ell$情况下的局部-全局兼容性，将Varma的结果推广到了Betti上同调中出现的挠类上。论证结合了Scholze的构造与$\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}$系数的$p$-adic一般线性群表示理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Varma关于局部-全局兼容性的结果，使其适用于Betti上同调中的挠类情形，从而深化对自守群行列式在$p \neq \ell$条件下性质的理解。

Method: 将Scholze的自守群行列式构造与$\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}$系数的$p$-adic一般线性群表示理论相结合进行论证。

Result: 在$p \neq \ell$的条件下，证明了Scholze构造的自守群行列式满足局部-全局兼容性，且该结果覆盖了Betti上同调中的挠类。

Conclusion: 成功将局部-全局兼容性结果推广到更一般的挠类情形，为自守群行列式的进一步研究提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove local-global compatibility results at $p \neq \ell$ for the automorphic group determinants constructed by Scholze, generalising the result of Varma to torsion classes appearing in Betti cohomology. Our argument combines the construction of Scholze with the theory of representations of $p$-adic general linear groups with $\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}$-coefficients.

</details>


### [150] [An Efficient Algorithm for Estimating Prime Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31761)
*Artur Samojluk,Artur Siemaszko*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于广义三角数结构化非均匀划分的高效素数计数函数π(x)近似算法，实现摊销O(1)更新复杂度和O(√x)总复杂度，在10^19范围内与已知值高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有素数计数函数的精确计算复杂度较高，在大规模数论应用中需要快速重复估计π(x)，但现有近似方法（如经典解析近似）评估方案复杂，缺乏高效的增量计算框架。

Method: 采用广义三角数导出的结构化非均匀划分策略，设计增量估计算法，使得每次更新仅需局部计算；通过大量数值实验获得的校正项进一步提升近似精度。

Result: 算法实现摊销O(1)更新复杂度和O(√x)总复杂度；在高达10^19的数值测试中与已知π(x)值吻合良好，精度与经典解析近似方法相当。

Conclusion: 该框架在需要快速重复估计π(x)的大规模计算数论应用中具有实用价值，提供了一种更简单的增量评估方案。

Abstract: We propose an efficient algorithm for approximating the prime counting function $π(x)$ using a structured non-uniform partition derived from generalized triangular numbers. The method yields an incremental estimator whose updates require only local computations, resulting in amortized $O(1)$ update complexity and total complexity $O(\sqrt x)$.
  A correction term obtained through extensive numerical experimentation significantly improves the approximation accuracy. Computational tests for values up to $10^{19}$
  show strong agreement with known values of $π(x)$, with accuracy comparable to classical analytic approximations, while maintaining a substantially simpler incremental evaluation scheme. The proposed framework may be useful in large-scale computational number theory applications requiring fast repeated estimates of $π(x)$.

</details>


### [151] [Construction of Generically Ordinary Families of Hyperelliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31783)
*Hui June Zhu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对所有g≥2和非零代数整数α，曲线族y²=x^d+αx+t（d=2g+1或2g+2）在满足特定素数界条件下是generic ordinary的，推广了Katz关于y²=x^d-dx+t的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Katz在2018年的讲座中猜想曲线族y²=x^d-dx+t在t-直线上对足够大的素数p是generic ordinary的。本文旨在推广这一猜想到更一般的曲线族，并给出具体的素数界值。

Method: 本文通过代数几何和算术几何方法，考虑一般曲线族C_α: y²=x^d+αx+t，其中d=2g+1或2g+2，α为非零代数整数。利用素数的上界P⁺(d)（d为奇数时d²-4d+2，d为偶数时(d²-3d+2)/2）来刻画条件。

Result: 对于每个g≥2和每个非零代数整数α，曲线族在任意素数p>P⁺(d)下是generic ordinary的，前提是α模每个p之上的素数不为零。给出了明确的上界表达式。

Conclusion: 本文成功推广了Katz猜想，从特定的d-dx形式推广到了更一般的+αx形式，并给出了具体的素数界条件，为理解模p下曲线的ordinary性质提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Katz conjectured in a 2018 lecture that the family of curves $y^2=x^d-dx+t$ over the $t$-line is generically ordinary for all sufficiently large primes $p$. We prove that, for every $g\ge 2$ and every nonzero algebraic integer $α$, the genus-$g$ families $C_α: y^2=x^d+αx+t$ where $d\in\{2g+1, 2g+2\}$ are generically ordinary at every prime $p>P^+(d)$, provided that $α$ is nonzero modulo every prime above $p$. The bound $P^+(d)=d^2-4d+2$ if $d$ is odd, and $P^+(d)=(d^2-3d+2)/2$ if $d$ is even.

</details>


### [152] [Galois Extensions via Finiteness of Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31900)
*Nikolaos Marmaridis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自同构群轨道作用的伽罗瓦理论重构，通过域上H-轨道的有限性统一刻画了代数性、可分性、正规性及扩张次数，并给出了固定域的有效计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统伽罗瓦理论对域扩张性质的刻画依赖于多项式分裂域等代数对象，缺乏从群作用轨道这一更直接的几何/组合视角的统一理解。本文旨在建立轨道理论与域扩张代数性质之间的内在联系。

Method: 利用自同构群子群H在域E上的自然作用，研究H-轨道的性质（有限性、长度有界性）与域扩张E/E^H的代数性质（代数性、可分性、正规性、有限性）之间的对应关系；通过轨道上初等对称多项式构造极小多项式和固定域。

Result: (1) α在E^H上代数当且仅当其H-轨道有限，且极小多项式可显式构造为轨道上线性因子的乘积（自动保证可分性）；(2) E/E^H是伽罗瓦扩张当且仅当所有H-轨道有限长，有限伽罗瓦扩张当且仅当轨道长度一致有界；(3) 恢复Artin引理作为特例；(4) 对单扩张F(α)/F，子群H的固定域可通过α的H-轨道上初等对称多项式显式计算。

Conclusion: 该轨道理论框架统一了伽罗瓦理论中多个核心概念的刻画，并提供了计算固定域的有效算法，为域论和伽罗瓦理论提供了新的视角和实用工具。

Abstract: We present an orbit--theoretic reformulation of Galois theory based on the natural action of automorphism groups on fields. Given a field $\mathbf{E}$ and a subgroup $H$ of the automorphism group $\mathrm{Aut}(\mathbf{E})$, we show that algebraic properties of the extension $\mathbf{E}/\mathbf{E}^H$, where $\mathbf{E}^H$ denotes the fixed field of $H$, are encoded in the $H$-orbits arising from the action of $H$ on $\mathbf{E}$.
  An element $α\in \mathbf{E}$ is algebraic over $\mathbf{E}^H$ if and only if its $H$--orbit is finite. In that case, its minimal polynomial can be explicitly constructed as the product of linear factors over its orbit --a construction that also ensures separability.
  At the level of field extensions, we prove that $\mathbf{E}/\mathbf{E}^H$ is Galois if and only if all $H$--orbits have finite length, and that $\mathbf{E}/\mathbf{E}^H$ is a finite Galois extension if and only if the lengths of the $H$--orbits are bounded above. This provides a unified orbit--theoretic characterization of algebraicity, separability, normality, and degree. Artin's Lemma is recovered as a direct consequence of this framework.
  Finally, we show that for simple extensions, the fixed field under a subgroup $H$ of $\mathrm{Aut}(\mathbf{F}(α)/\mathbf{F})$ can be described explicitly by evaluating elementary symmetric polynomials on the $H$--orbit of $α$, provided this orbit is finite. This leads to an effective method for computing fixed fields directly from orbit data. A classical example is included to illustrate the approach.

</details>


### [153] [EKOR and BT stratifications for basic unramified $\mathrm{GU}(1,n-1)$ Rapoport-Zink spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31940)
*Joseph Muller*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了无分歧G U(1,n-1)Rapoport-Zink空间上Ekedahl-Kottwitz-Oort-Rapoport分层与Bruhat-Tits分层之间的关系，证明了每个基本EKOR层是一族精细Deligne-Lusztig簇的不交并，并确定了完全包含在基本轨迹中的KR层，以及证明了某些EKOR层闭包的不可约分量的光滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立EKOR分层与Bruhat-Tits分层之间的联系，理解Rapoport-Zink空间的几何结构。

Method: 通过证明每个基本EKOR层是一族由显式定义的精细Deligne-Lusztig簇的不交并，建立两种分层之间的联系。

Result: (1) 每个基本EKOR层是精细Deligne-Lusztig簇的不交并；(2) 确定了完全包含在基本轨迹中的KR层；(3) 证明了某些EKOR层闭包的不可约分量的光滑性。

Conclusion: 成功建立了两种分层之间的精确对应关系，并获得了关于KR层和EKOR层闭包不可约分量的重要几何性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the relation between the Ekedahl-Kottwitz-Oort-Rapoport stratification and the Bruhat-Tits stratification on the unramified $\mathrm{GU}(1,n-1)$ Rapoport-Zink space with arbitrary parahoric level. More precisely, we prove that every basic EKOR stratum is a disjoint union of copies of a fine Deligne-Lusztig variety which is explicitly defined. As a consequence, we also determine which KR strata are entirely contained in the basic locus, and we prove the smoothness of the irreducible components of the closure of certain EKOR strata.

</details>

### [154] [Persistence, Thresholds, and Trait Composition in a Regulated Mutation-Selection Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31146)
*Phil. Pollett*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 研究了一个双性状种群模型，在突变、选择和密度依赖调控下，分析了灭绝、持久性和长期性状组成的阈值条件，揭示了突变诱导的有效死亡率、继承主导突变时的建立阈值，以及拟平衡态下的性状相关转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解突变、选择和资源限制如何共同决定种群的生态持存性和进化结果。

Method: 确定性大种群极限得到耦合逻辑增长与突变-选择动力学的非线性系统；扩散近似用于描述拟平衡行为。

Result: 突变诱导的有效死亡率决定种群能否维持；继承主导突变时出现第二个阈值，种群建立取决于初始性状组成和增长率；系统表现出长寿命拟平衡行为；性状相关性符号发生转变。

Conclusion: 突变、选择和资源限制三者共同塑造生态持久性与进化结果，模型为理解这一耦合过程提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study a population model in which individuals carry one of two traits and evolve under mutation, selection, and density-dependent regulation. A deterministic large-population limit yields a nonlinear system coupling logistic growth with mutation-selection dynamics. We identify threshold conditions governing extinction, persistence, and long-term trait composition. In particular, mutation induces an effective mortality rate that determines whether the population can be sustained. When inheritance dominates mutation, a second threshold emerges: population establishment depends on initial trait composition as well as overall growth rates. Although extinction ultimately occurs, the system typically exhibits long-lived quasi-equilibrium behaviour. A diffusion approximation provides a tractable description of this, and reveals a transition in the sign of trait correlations. The model thus illustrates how mutation, selection, and resource limitation jointly shape both ecological persistence and evolutionary outcomes.

</details>


### [155] [A conceptual model for the evo-devo role of transposable elements and its implications for the ageing phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31733)
*Alessandro Fontana*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出转座子（TEs）在可演化的体细胞衰老理论中扮演双重角色：早期被表观遗传严格抑制以维持稳定，后期因年龄相关的表观遗传变化而释放，导致转录随机性和衰老表型，同时为基因调控网络提供创新来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨转座子在可演化体细胞衰老理论模型中的作用，将转座子整合到进化、发育和衰老的统一框架中。

Method: 基于可演化体细胞衰老理论，分析转座子通过提供转录因子结合位点促进基因调控网络扩张与多样化，以及其受表观遗传调控在发育早期被抑制、在晚年被释放的机制。

Result: 转座子在发育早期被表观遗传严格抑制，在晚年因遗传驱动的年龄相关表观遗传变化而释放，导致转录伪随机性和衰老表型；同时为调控创新提供基础。

Conclusion: 转座子整合到统一理论中，解释了其在调控创新与年龄相关衰退中的双重作用。

Abstract: The Evolvable Soma Theory of Ageing is a recently proposed model that frames development as a continuous process of change accompanying organisms throughout the lifespan. This process is driven by developmental genes which encode epigenetic changes on target cells, whereas ageing reflects the expression of late-acting modifications, that are subject to ongoing evolutionary optimisation and function as somatic "experiments" to explore phenotypic novelty. In this work we examine the role of transposable elements in the model. Our proposal acknowledges that these elements facilitate the expansion and diversification of gene regulatory networks by providing transcription factor binding sites. To minimise disruption, their regulatory activity is tightly repressed by epigenetic mechanisms during early development, which may be progressively released by genetically driven, age-associated epigenetic changes in later life, thereby contributing to transcriptional pseudo-randomness and ageing-associated phenotypes. Within this framework, transposable elements are integrated into a unified view of evolution, development and ageing, providing a conceptual basis for their dual role in regulatory innovation and age-related decline.

</details>


### [156] [Nonlinear Feedbacks Between Host Behavior and Vector Adaptation in a Multi-Host Vector-Borne Disease Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31768)
*Shravani Shetgaonkar,Anupama Sharma*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个非线性多宿主传染病模型，研究蚊帐（ITN）使用与疾病传播之间的耦合动态，发现宿主基于收益的决策行为可导致系统出现鞍结分支、Hopf分支及临界相变，并揭示了主宿主完全覆盖蚊帐反而可能因媒介摄食行为适应性转移而增加次宿主患病率的反直觉效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蚊帐（ITN）是控制媒介传播疾病（VBD）有效且低成本的干预措施，但其使用取决于个体基于感知成本与感染风险的决策。现有研究较少关注个体决策与疾病流行率之间的非线性耦合及其在多宿主环境中的复杂动态，因此本文旨在建立模型以探索这一相互作用机制。

Method: 建立非线性多宿主VBD传播模型，将宿主的ITN采用行为建模为基于收益的决策过程，并与疾病流行率形成非线性耦合；同时将媒介偏好建模为ITN覆盖率的函数。通过阈值R₀和R_c分析无病平衡点与地方病平衡点的存在性与局部稳定性，并利用分支分析识别鞍结分支和Hopf分支的条件。

Result: 系统表现出丰富的动力学行为：感知成本与感染风险的交互可诱发临界相变，包括从稳定地方病状态向持续周期振荡的跃迁。此外发现一个反直觉效应——主宿主完全覆盖ITN反而可能因媒介摄食行为的适应性转移而导致次宿主患病率上升。

Conclusion: 个体决策与疾病传播的耦合可产生复杂的非线性动态，包括临界相变和反直觉的干预效果。在制定VBD控制策略时，需考虑多宿主环境中媒介行为适应性变化带来的间接效应。

Abstract: Insecticide-treated nets (ITN) are an effective and low-cost intervention for controlling vector-borne disease (VBD), however, their use depends on individual decisions based on perceived cost and risk of infection. This study investigates a nonlinear multi-host model for the transmission of VBD with endogenous strategic control. We assume that hosts' adoption of ITN emerges from the payoff-based decision-making, creating a nonlinear coupling with disease prevalence. We model vector preference as a function of ITN coverage to probe the complex interplay among individual choices, disease prevalence, and its control in a multi-host setting. The qualitative behavior of the system is characterized by the thresholds $R_0$ and $R_c$, which determine the existence and local stability of the disease-free and endemic equilibria. The system exhibits rich dynamical behavior; hence, we provide a bifurcation analysis identifying the conditions for saddle-node and Hopf bifurcations. Our results demonstrate that the interaction between the perceived cost of ITN and the infection risk can induce critical transitions, including regime shift from stable endemic states to sustained periodic oscillations. Furthermore, we identify a counterintuitive effect whereby complete ITN adoption by the primary host can increase the overall prevalence in the secondary host due to adaptive shifts of vector feeding behavior.

</details>


### [157] [Invasion with size-dependent dispersion range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31942)
*Ulysse Marquis*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了同质化菌落模型，引入与菌落大小相关的扩散距离（标度指数μ），推导了广义动力学方程，发现了线性扩张、幂律增长、指数增长和有限时间爆发等不同生长相。通过空间显式物理模型验证发现，理论虽能正确捕捉周长标度，但无法预测体积标度，原因是主菌落形态的圆对称性被破坏。当μ>0.7时，物理模型中存在理论未捕获的持续卫星种群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准同质化菌落模型假设扩散距离与入侵种群大小无关，但现实中的生物入侵、转移性生长和城市扩张中，扩散距离往往依赖于种群规模。本研究旨在放松这一假设，探索大小依赖的扩散如何改变入侵动力学。

Method: 将标准同质化菌落模型扩展为大小依赖扩散：主菌落线性尺寸r发射次级菌落的距离为r^μ (0≤μ≤1)。推导广义动力学方程，构建生长相图，并通过空间显式物理模型进行数值验证。

Result: 1) 随μ值不同，主菌落呈现线性扩张(μ=0)、幂律增长、指数增长和有限时间爆发(μ=1)等不同生长相；2) 理论正确预测周长标度，但未能预测体积标度，因主菌落形态失去圆对称性；3) 当μ<1时，主菌落外种群分数按幂律衰减至0；μ=1时存在宏观卫星种群；4) μ>0.7时物理模型显示理论未捕获的持续卫星种群。

Conclusion: 将扩散距离耦合到种群大小会从根本上改变入侵动力学，同质化菌落方法在周长标度上有效，但在体积标度和卫星种群预测上存在局限。研究结果对生物入侵、转移性生长和城市扩张具有启示意义。

Abstract: The coalescing colony model provides a minimal framework for biological invasions with long-range dispersion. In its standard formulation, the dispersion range is assumed independent of the size of the invading population. Here, we relax this assumption and consider size-dependent dispersal: a main colony of linear size $r$ emits secondary colonies at distance $r^μ$, with $0 \leq μ\leq 1$. We derive the generalized dynamical equations for this extended model and map out the growth phase diagram for the leading order contribution. Depending on $μ$, the main colony exhibits distinct regimes: linear expansion, power-law growth, exponential regime and finite-time blow-up. We confront these theoretical predictions with a spatially explicit physical model. While the coalescing colony approach correctly captures the scaling of the perimeter, it fails to predict the scaling of the volume. We trace this discrepancy to an effective breakdown of circular symmetry in the morphology of the main colony. Finally, we quantify temporal evolution of the population fraction residing outside of the main colony. The coalescing colony model predicts its decay to~$0$ like a power-law when~$μ<1$, and a macroscopic amount of the population remains in the secondary colonies at~$μ=1$. Simulations of the physical model reveal a persistent satellite population not captured by the theory at~$μ>μ^*\approx 0.7$. Broadly, our findings highlight how coupling dispersal range to population size fundamentally alters invasion dynamics, with implications for biological invasions, metastatic growth, and urban expansion.

</details>

### [158] [Estimating the Effect of Timing on Coupon Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30664)
*Deddy Jobson*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用因果推断框架，通过“自然随机对照实验”来评估优惠券发送时机对用户参与度的影响，无需昂贵的实时触发系统或专门AB测试，并在企业内部入职活动和公开数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优惠券激励是常用的营销工具，其效果受多种因素影响，其中时机尤为关键。然而，在用户与平台交互的关键时刻实时发送优惠券需要昂贵的软件支持。作者希望在不依赖高成本AB测试系统的前提下，验证优惠券在关键时刻发送是否更有效。

Method: 提出一个因果推断框架，利用“自然随机对照实验”（即观察性研究中近似随机分配的处理）来估计优惠券发送时机的因果效应。该方法无需专用的实时事件触发分发系统或专门的AB测试，通过对自然产生的实验条件进行因果建模，衡量在用户旅程关键节点发送优惠券的效果。

Result: 在公司内部用户入职优惠券活动中，该框架成功测量了正确时机发送优惠券的效果，并引导了正确的数据驱动决策。此外，在公开数据集上的用户留存活动中验证了框架的泛化能力和可重复性。

Conclusion: 提出的因果推断框架能够有效且低成本地评估优惠券发送时机对用户行为的影响，有助于企业在无需昂贵基础设施的情况下做出数据驱动的营销决策，且框架具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: The coupon incentive is one of the most common tools marketers use to court users to engage with a business at various stages of the customer life cycle. A variety of factors can affect the effectiveness of a coupon incentive on users, timing being one of them. We hypothesize that coupons can be more effective when delivered at critical times in the customer journey, right when a user is engaging with the platform. Verifying such a hypothesis would typically require real time event-triggered coupon distribution software that may be too expensive to implement. In this paper, we propose a framework in which we apply causal inference on "natural randomized control trial experiments" to measure the effectiveness of sending coupons at the right time to users without requiring a dedicated AB test. We demonstrate the usefulness of our framework in the case of a user onboarding coupon campaign held in our company and show how the results can lead to correct data-driven decisions for the business. Furthermore, in order to test the generalizability of our framework, and to make our research more reproducible, we apply our framework on a user retention campaign with a publicly available dataset.

</details>


### [159] [Topological reconstruction of Rubin multiple imputation via coarse proximity, Seifert van Kampen gluing and Hurewicz invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30684)
*Arturo Tozzi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种拓扑重建方法，通过粗几何与代数拓扑工具（粗邻近图、Seifert van Kampen粘合、Hurewicz型不变量）对Rubin多重插补生成的完整数据集集合进行全局结构分析，在合成生物医学数据上验证了该方法能揭示多重插补空间的稳定拓扑特征与缺失率相关的结构转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rubin多重插补虽能生成合理的数据补齐以处理不确定性与统计变异性，但对其全局组织结构缺乏洞察，需要一种能从大尺度刻画不同插补数据集之间关系的方法。

Method: 将每次插补表示为一个统计特征坐标构成的点，构建重建空间；利用粗邻近性定义大尺度邻域生成图（节点为完整数据集，边连接足够相似的插补）；通过Seifert van Kampen粘合解释局部重建如何组装为全局一致结构；使用Hurewicz型不变量量化持久连通模式。在带控制缺失率的合成成人心脏代谢生物医学队列数据上验证。

Result: 多重插补生成了具有明显连通模式的结构化空间而非均匀解云；拓扑描述符在局部数值变异下保持稳定；缺失率增加导致重建空间连通性转变及重建精度逐步恶化。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至生物与社会网络、系统医学、生态建模等领域，其中大规模结构组织有助于可靠推断。

Abstract: Rubin multiple imputation (MI) generates plausible data completions to account for uncertainty and statistical variability but provides little insight into their global organization. We introduce a topological reconstruction approach that complements MI by examining the ensemble of completed datasets. Individual imputations are represented as points in a reconstruction space whose coordinates summarize statistical properties. Concepts from coarse geometry and algebraic topology are then used to characterize relationships among alternative imputations across multiple scales. Coarse proximity (CP) defines large-scale neighborhoods, generating graphs in which nodes represent completed datasets and edges connect sufficiently similar imputations. Seifert van Kampen gluing provides a conceptual interpretation of how local reconstructions assemble into globally coherent structures, whereas Hurewicz-type invariants quantify persistent connectivity patterns. Synthetic multivariate biomedical datasets representing adult cardiometabolic cohorts were generated with controlled missingness levels. Multiple stochastic imputations were projected into the reconstruction space and analyzed through CP graphs, connected components, cycle descriptors and scale-dependent topological measures. MI generated structured spaces with distinct connectivity patterns rather than homogeneous clouds of solutions. Topological descriptors remained stable despite local numerical variability, whereas increasing missingness produced transitions in reconstruction-space connectivity together with progressive deterioration of reconstruction accuracy. Our approach could be applied to biological and social networks, systems medicine, ecological modeling and other domains in which large-scale structural organization contributes to reliable inference.

</details>


### [160] [Spatial Dependence in the Self-Response: Spatial Dependence, Modeling, and Operational Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30816)
*Emanuel Ben-David*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用2010年人口普查的71,076个美国人口普查区数据，系统比较了空间自回归模型家族在低响应分数（LRS）建模中的表现。研究发现OLS残差存在显著空间自相关（Moran's I=0.399），残差依赖主要为误差型而非全局内生滞后。空间区块交叉验证显示误差族模型（SEM/SDEM）泛化能力最佳，而AIC最优的SDM模型样本外表现最弱。SDEM模型可在吸收空间依赖的同时将邻域人口效应解释为局部溢出，为官方统计提供了可操作且可解释的建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国人口普查局的低响应分数（LRS）是识别需要额外 outreach 和追访区域的核心规划工具。LRS采用普通最小二乘法（OLS）构建以保持可解释性，但这种透明性忽略了空间结构的影响。官方统计面临一个关键问题：在控制本区协变量后，剩余的空间结构有多大、呈现何种形式？这直接关系到LRS预测的准确性和泛化能力。

Method: 使用2010年人口普查的71,076个美国人口普查区邮件不回复率和25个Erdman-Bates LRS预测变量，在女王邻接权重下比较完整的空间自回归模型家族（包括OLS、SAR、SEM、SDM、SDEM等）。采用随机交叉验证和空间区块交叉验证两种策略进行模型验证，并通过Moran's I检验、形式诊断和模型比较确定空间依赖的类型（误差型 vs. 全局内生滞后）。

Result: OLS残差存在强空间自相关（I=0.399）。形式诊断和模型比较表明剩余依赖主要是误差型而非全局内生滞后过程。空间Durbin模型（SDM）在样本内AIC最优，但空间区块交叉验证推翻了这一排序：误差族模型（SEM/SDEM）泛化能力最佳，AIC最优的SDM样本外表现最弱。稳健性检验表明结论不受权重定义影响，也不是普查区面积异方差性的伪像。

Conclusion: LRS风格的响应模型应使用空间交叉验证而非仅依赖样本内拟合度进行评估；局部邻域上下文可以具有操作意义，无需引入全局响应传染机制；SDEM在吸收空间依赖的同时将邻域人口效应表示为局部溢出，是兼具可解释性和预测性能的折中方案。

Abstract: The U.S.\ Census Bureau's Low Response Score (LRS) is a central planning instrument for identifying places likely to require additional self-response outreach and nonresponse follow-up. The published LRS is intentionally interpretable: it is built from tract-level covariates using an ordinary least squares specification. That transparency, however, leaves open an important question for official statistics: how much spatial structure remains after the own-tract covariates have done their work, and what form does that structure take? Using the observed 2010 Census mail non-return rate for 71,076 U.S. census tracts and the twenty-five Erdman--Bates LRS predictors, this paper compares the full spatial autoregressive model family under queen-contiguity weights and validates the leading candidates with both random and spatial-block cross-validation. OLS leaves strong residual spatial autocorrelation ($I=0.399$). Formal diagnostics and model comparisons indicate that the remaining dependence is primarily error-type rather than a global endogenous lag process. Although the spatial Durbin model minimizes in-sample AIC, spatial-block validation reverses that ranking: the error-family models (SEM/SDEM) generalize best, while the AIC-best SDM is weakest out of sample. The SDEM provides an interpretable middle ground, absorbing residual spatial dependence while representing neighborhood demographic effects as local spillovers. Robustness checks show that these conclusions are invariant to the weights definition and are not an artifact of tract-size-driven heteroskedasticity. The results suggest that LRS-style response models should be evaluated with spatial validation, not only in-sample fit, and that local neighborhood context can be operationally meaningful without invoking a global response-contagion mechanism.

</details>


### [161] [Bayesian Uncertainty Quantification for Ranked Choice Voting Polls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31022)
*Evan T. R. Rosenman,Jason Liang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯框架，用于量化排序选择投票（RCV）选举民调中的不确定性，通过估计领先候选人的获胜概率，并在2021年纽约市民主党市长初选和2022年阿拉斯加州美国众议院席位特别选举中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCV选举中，候选人的获胜不能用一个单一总体参数表示，因此传统民调的误差范围概念难以定义；且候选人获胜可能依赖于选票支持的分布及其他候选人被淘汰的顺序，现有民调不确定性度量方法未能清晰量化这些路径依赖的结果。

Method: 采用贝叶斯框架，将问题转化为估计每位领先候选人获胜概率的问题；利用共轭先验关系，基于民调结果条件性地估计这些概率。

Result: 使用2021年纽约市民主党市长初选和2022年阿拉斯加州美国众议院席位特别选举的投票记录，展示了传统频率学派不确定性量化在RCV民调中的挑战；并利用Data for Progress对纽约市初选的民调数据验证了所提方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的贝叶斯框架能有效量化RCV选举民调中的不确定性，克服了传统方法难以处理路径依赖和单一参数缺失的问题。

Abstract: Ranked choice voting (RCV) is a popular alternative voting method in which voters are asked to list their favored candidates in preference order, rather than vote for a single candidate. When these ballots are tabulated, candidates are successively eliminated, and their votes are reallocated to each voter's next-preferred choice. The process continues until a candidate commands a majority of the active ballots and is declared the winner. As RCV gains wider adoption, the method poses novel challenges for pollsters. Unlike plurality elections, the event that a candidate wins cannot be expressed in terms of a single population parameter. Hence, the basic concept of a margin-of-error is not straightforward to define. Moreover, a candidate's ability to win may depend on both their support across the ballot and the order in which other candidates are eliminated. Existing measures of sampling uncertainty for polls of RCV elections do not clearly quantify these path-dependent outcomes. Here, we propose a simple, Bayesian framework to quantify uncertainty in polls of RCV elections. We cast the problem as one of estimating win probabilities for each leading candidate, and leverage a simple conjugacy relationship to estimate these probabilities conditional on the poll results. We include applied analyses involving two prominent ranked choice voting elections: the 2021 New York City Democratic mayoral primary, in which Eric Adams narrowly defeated Kathryn Garcia in the final round; and the 2022 special election to Alaska's U.S. House seat, in which Mary Peltola was elected despite not being a Condorcet winner. Using the cast vote records from both elections, we demonstrate some challenges of traditional frequentist uncertainty quantification in RCV polls. We also demonstrate the utility of our approach using a poll of the NYC primary obtained from the polling firm Data for Progress.

</details>
